    ── Created .sources/kadri-2024-hydroacoustics/code/conditional_hypothesis_tests.py +456
      #!/usr/bin/env python3
      """Conditional source-side tests on publication vectors, never a likelihood.

      Reuses the canonical extraction, score, geometry and FFT/IAAFT primitives.
      This focused control is deliberately outside the estimator/Cargo workspace.
      """
      from __future__ import annotations

      import csv
      import hashlib
      import json
      import math
      import resource
      import time
      from datetime import datetime, timezone
      from pathlib import Path

      import matplotlib
      matplotlib.use('Agg')
      import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
      import numpy as np
      from scipy.ndimage import maximum_filter1d
      from scipy.optimize import brentq
      from scipy.signal import find_peaks

      import event_pair_analysis as base
      import filter_sensitivity as airgun
      import publication_trace_score_overview as overview
      import aligned_detection_analysis as aligned
      import two_station_correlation_scan as scan

      ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
      DATA = ROOT / 'data'
      OUT = ROOT / 'outputs' / 'conditional-hypothesis-tests'
      CFG_PATH = DATA / 'conditional-hypothesis-config.json'
      CFG = json.loads(CFG_PATH.read_text())
      BASECFG = json.loads((DATA / 'event-pair-analysis-config.json').read_text())
      SCREEN = BASECFG['trace_screen']
      DT = SCREEN['regular_grid_dt_s']
      STEP = CFG['pair_grid_dt_s']
      MIDNIGHT = base.parse_utc('2014-03-08T00:00:00Z').timestamp()
      TREF = base.parse_utc(CFG['impact_reference_utc']).timestamp() - MIDNIGHT
      STATIONS = {'CL': (-34.892, 114.141), 'DG': (-7.639, 72.484)}
      PURPLE, RED, GREY = '#542078', '#bd3049', '#a6a8ad'


      def quant(x):
          return [float(v) for v in np.quantile(x, [.025, .5, .975])]


      def utc(sec):
          if not np.isfinite(sec):
              return 'not covered'
          return datetime.fromtimestamp(MIDNIGHT + float(sec), timezone.utc).strftime('%H:%M:%S')


      def pvalue(null, value):
          return float((1 + np.count_nonzero(np.asarray(null) >= value)) / (len(null) + 1))


      def save_csv(name, rows):
          keys = list(dict.fromkeys(k for row in rows for k in row))
          with (OUT / name).open('w', newline='') as f:
              w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=keys)
              w.writeheader()
              w.writerows(rows)


      def distance(lat, lon, station):
          return base.haversine_distance_km(np.asarray(lat), np.asarray(lon), STATIONS[station], 6371.0088)


      def arc():
          xy = np.array(json.loads(base.ARC_PATH.read_text())['features'][0]['geometry']['coordinates'])
          xy = xy[(xy[:, 1] < -15) & (xy[:, 0] > 70)]
          order = np.argsort(xy[:, 1])
          return xy[order]


      def dense_arc():
          xy = arc()
          lat = np.linspace(-45, -20, 10001)
          lon = np.interp(lat, xy[:, 1], xy[:, 0])
          return lat, lon


      def drift_grids():
          path = ROOT.parent / 'pleiades-bran2016-forward-inversion/outputs/conditional-impact-density.csv'
          rows = list(csv.DictReader(path.open()))
          result = {}
          for label, model in [('BRAN', 'bran2016'), ('OSCAR', 'oscar_v2_final')]:
              selected = [r for r in rows if r['current_model'] == model]
              a = {key: np.array([float(r[key]) for r in selected]) for key in
                   ['latitude', 'longitude', 'probabilityMass', 'densityPerKm2']}
              a['probabilityMass'] /= a['probabilityMass'].sum()
              order = np.argsort(a['densityPerKm2'])[::-1]
              cum = np.cumsum(a['probabilityMass'][order])
              a['hpd_rank'] = np.empty(len(order))
              a['hpd_rank'][order] = cum
              a['hpd95'] = a['hpd_rank'] <= .95
              a['hpd95'][order[np.searchsorted(cum, .95)]] = True
              a['mode'] = int(np.argmax(a['densityPerKm2']))
              result[label] = a
          return result, path


      def filter_control(traces, rng):
          """Unaligned, leave-target-and-neighbours-out subspaces avoid all-cycle templates.

          Rank zero means the identity control; rank one is median pulse only.
          Fixed pulse centres are estimated once on unmodified data. Injections test
          subtraction conditional on these centres, not end-to-end source recovery.
          """
          fc = json.loads((DATA / 'filter-sensitivity-config.json').read_text())
          rows, cache, periods, injection_rows = [], {}, {}, []
          for panel_name in ['d', 'e']:
              panel = overview.load_h08_panel(panel_name, fc)
              fc['event_utc'] = base.iso_utc(panel.start_epoch_s + 300)
              geom = airgun.fit_shot_geometry(panel, fc)
              centres = geom.observed_centres_s
              centres = centres[(centres > 10) & (centres < 590)]
              tx = np.arange(-4.5, 4.5 + DT/2, DT)
              values = np.array([np.interp(c + tx, panel.time_s, panel.pressure_pa) for c in centres])
              periods[panel_name] = float(geom.fitted_period_s)
              inject = []
              for sigma in [.2, .5, 1.0]:
                  for offset in [-3, -1, 0, 1, 3]:
                      for frequency in [0, 1, 3]:
                          s = np.exp(-.5*((tx-offset)/sigma)**2)*np.cos(2*np.pi*frequency*(tx-offset))
                          s /= np.sqrt(np.sum(s*s))
                          inject.append(s)
              inject = np.array(inject)
              residuals = {rank: values.copy() for rank in CFG['filter_ranks']}
              retained = {rank: [] for rank in CFG['filter_ranks']}
              for i in range(len(values)):
                  train = values[np.abs(np.arange(len(values))-i) > 1]
                  centre = np.median(train, axis=0)
                  _, _, vt = np.linalg.svd(train - centre, full_matrices=False)
                  q, _ = np.linalg.qr(np.column_stack([centre, vt[:19].T]))
                  for rank in CFG['filter_ranks']:
                      basis = q[:, :rank]
                      residuals[rank][i] = values[i] - basis @ (basis.T @ values[i])
                      ret = 1 - np.sum((inject @ basis)**2, axis=1)
                      retained[rank].extend(ret.tolist())
              for rank, res in residuals.items():
                  original_rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(values**2, axis=1))
                  residual_rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(res**2, axis=1))
                  retained_a = np.array(retained[rank])
                  rows.append({'panel': panel_name, 'rank': rank, 'cycles': len(centres),
                               'median_rms_removed_fraction': float(1-np.median(residual_rms/original_rms)),
                               'median_matched_amplitude_retention': float(np.median(retained_a)),
                               'q10_matched_amplitude_retention': float(np.quantile(retained_a, .1))})
                  full = panel.pressure_pa.copy()
                  for i, centre in enumerate(centres):
                      use = np.abs(panel.time_s - centre) <= 4.5
                      full[use] = np.interp(panel.time_s[use] - centre, tx, res[i])
                  cache[(panel_name, rank)] = full
                  for val in retained_a[::len(inject)]:
                      injection_rows.append({'panel': panel_name, 'rank': rank, 'example_matched_retention': float(val)})
          feasible = []
          for rank in CFG['filter_ranks']:
              r = [a for a in rows if a['rank'] == rank]
              if all(a['median_matched_amplitude_retention'] >= CFG['filter_median_injection_retention_minimum']
                     and a['q10_matched_amplitude_retention'] >= CFG['filter_lower_decile_retention_minimum'] for a in r):
                  feasible.append((np.mean([a['median_rms_removed_fraction'] for a in r]), rank))
          chosen = max(feasible)[1]
          save_csv('filter-trade-off.csv', rows)
          return chosen, rows, {p: cache[(p, chosen)] for p in ['d', 'e']}, periods


      def score_series(traces, names, residual=None, score_override=None):
          start = traces[names[0]].start_epoch_s - MIDNIGHT
          end = traces[names[-1]].start_epoch_s - MIDNIGHT + 600
          t = np.arange(start, end, STEP)
          z = np.zeros(t.size)
          valid = np.zeros(t.size, bool)
          for p in names:
              tr = traces[p]
              local = tr.epoch_s - MIDNIGHT
              s = score_override[p] if score_override is not None else (
                  base.score_signal(residual[p], SCREEN) if residual and p in residual else tr.score)
              use = (t >= local[0]+10) & (t < local[0]+590)
              z[use] = np.interp(t[use], local, s)
              valid[use] = True
          return scan.Series(t, z, valid)


      def pressure_peak(energy, ranges):
          """Brown et al. Eq. 2 explosion-equivalent sensitivity; not validated paths."""
          return 52.4e6 * (np.asarray(ranges)*1000 / (np.asarray(energy)/4.184e6)**(1/3))**(-1.13)


      def mc_banks(grids, rng, cl, dg):
          n = CFG['samples_per_hypothesis']
          banks, summaries, energy_rows = {}, {}, []
          rate = rng.triangular(*CFG['holland_vertical_rate_fpm'], size=n)
          height = rng.uniform(*CFG['holland_altitude_ft'], size=n)
          delay = 60*height/rate
          w = rate*.3048/60
          vh = rng.uniform(*CFG['horizontal_speed_m_s'], size=n)
          normal_energy = .5*CFG['mass_kg']*w*w
          total_energy = normal_energy + .5*CFG['mass_kg']*vh*vh
          coupling = 10**rng.uniform(*CFG['coupling_working_log10_bounds'], size=n)
          broad = 10**rng.uniform(*CFG['coupling_broad_log10_bounds'], size=n)
          eac = normal_energy*coupling
          lat_arc, lon_arc = dense_arc()
          # Uniform arclength, not uniform latitude; arc is a reference geometry only.
          dl = np.sqrt(np.diff(lat_arc)**2 + (np.diff(lon_arc)*np.cos(np.radians(lat_arc[:-1])))**2)
          along = np.r_[0, np.cumsum(dl)]
          u = rng.uniform(0, along[-1], n)
          lat = np.interp(u, along, lat_arc)
          lon = np.interp(u, along, lon_arc)
          az = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi, n)
          angular = vh*delay/6371008.8
          phi, lam = np.radians(lat), np.radians(lon)
          outphi = np.arcsin(np.sin(phi)*np.cos(angular)+np.cos(phi)*np.sin(angular)*np.cos(az))
          outlam = lam + np.arctan2(np.sin(az)*np.sin(angular)*np.cos(phi), np.cos(angular)-np.sin(phi)*np.sin(outphi))
          holland = {'lat': np.degrees(outphi), 'lon': np.degrees(outlam), 't0': TREF+delay}
          intervals = {'impact_delay_s': quant(delay), 'impact_time_s': quant(TREF+delay),
                       'vertical_speed_m_s': quant(w), 'normal_energy_j': quant(normal_energy),
                       'total_kinetic_energy_j': quant(total_energy), 'coupling': quant(coupling),
                       'coupled_energy_j': quant(eac), 'horizontal_displacement_km': quant(vh*delay/1000),
                       'hard_delay_s': [60*20000/25300, 60*40000/13800]}
          sensitivity = []
          for lower, upper in [(0, 40000), (10000, 20000), (20000, 40000)]:
              for law in ['uniform', 'triangular']:
                  r = rng.uniform(13800, 25300, n) if law == 'uniform' else rng.triangular(13800,19550,25300,n)
                  d = rng.uniform(lower, upper, n)*60/r
                  sensitivity.append({'altitude_ft': f'{lower}–{upper}', 'rate_prior': law,
                                      'q025_delay_s': quant(d)[0], 'median_delay_s': quant(d)[1], 'q975_delay_s': quant(d)[2]})
          save_csv('holland-timing-sensitivity.csv', sensitivity)
          for label in ['BRAN', 'OSCAR', 'Holland']:
              if label == 'Holland':
                  b = holland
                  keep = (delay >= intervals['impact_delay_s'][0]) & (delay <= intervals['impact_delay_s'][2])
              else:
                  g = grids[label]
                  weights = g['probabilityMass']*g['hpd95']
                  indices = rng.choice(len(weights), n, p=weights/weights.sum())
                  b = {'lat': g['latitude'][indices], 'lon': g['longitude'][indices],
                       't0': rng.uniform(TREF, TREF+CFG['pleiades_source_duration_s'], n)}
                  keep = np.ones(n, bool)
              b['dcl'] = distance(b['lat'], b['lon'], 'CL')
              b['ddg'] = distance(b['lat'], b['lon'], 'DG')
              c1 = rng.uniform(*CFG['celerity_km_s'], n)
              c2 = rng.uniform(*CFG['celerity_km_s'], n)
              b['cl'] = b['t0'] + b['dcl']/c1 + rng.uniform(-5,5,n)
              b['dg'] = b['t0'] + b['ddg']/c2 + rng.uniform(-5,5,n)
              b['ic'] = np.rint((b['cl']-cl.time_epoch_s[0])/STEP).astype(int)
              b['id'] = np.rint((b['dg']-dg.time_epoch_s[0])/STEP).astype(int)
              cv = (b['ic']>=0)&(b['ic']<len(cl.values))
              dv = (b['id']>=0)&(b['id']<len(dg.values))
              cv &= cl.valid[np.clip(b['ic'],0,len(cl.values)-1)]
              dv &= dg.valid[np.clip(b['id'],0,len(dg.values)-1)]
              b['valid'] = keep & cv & dv
              b['keep'] = keep
              b['cv'] = cv & keep
              b['dv'] = dv & keep
              k = b['valid']
              b['pair_indices'] = np.where(k)[0]
              summaries[label] = {'cl_arrival_s': quant(b['cl'][keep]), 'dg_arrival_s': quant(b['dg'][keep]),
                                  'cl_range_km': quant(b['dcl']), 'dg_range_km': quant(b['ddg']),
                                  'arrival_lag_s': quant((b['dg']-b['cl'])[keep]),
                                  'CL_coverage_fraction': float(cv[keep].mean()), 'DG_coverage_fraction': float(dv[keep].mean()),
                                  'joint_coverage_fraction': float(k.sum()/keep.sum()), 'joint_sample_count': int(k.sum())}
              if label == 'Holland':
                  for coupling_name, energies in [('working',eac),('broad',normal_energy*broad)]:
                      for station, ranges in [('CL',b['dcl']),('DG',b['ddg'])]:
                          for loss in CFG['excess_loss_db_controls']:
                              peak = pressure_peak(energies,ranges)*10**(-loss/20)
                              # Independent energy-fluence bookkeeping, not a fitted propagation law.
                              f = energies*CFG['working_band_energy_fraction']*10**(-loss/10)/(2*np.pi*ranges*1000*CFG['ideal_duct_height_m'])
                              exposure = CFG['density_kg_m3']*CFG['impedance_sound_speed_m_s']*f
                              rms = np.sqrt(exposure/CFG['packet_duration_s'])
                              q = quant(peak)
                              energy_rows.append({'coupling_prior':coupling_name,'station':station,'excess_loss_db':loss,
                                                  'peak_q025_pa':q[0],'peak_median_pa':q[1],'peak_q975_pa':q[2],
                                                  'fluence_q025_j_m2':quant(f)[0],'fluence_median_j_m2':quant(f)[1],'fluence_q975_j_m2':quant(f)[2],
                                                  'ideal_duct_rms_q025_pa':quant(rms)[0],'ideal_duct_rms_median_pa':quant(rms)[1],'ideal_duct_rms_q975_pa':quant(rms)[2]})
              banks[label] = b
          save_csv('pressure-and-energy-sensitivity.csv', energy_rows)
          return banks, summaries, intervals, energy_rows


      def picked_scores(series):
          z = series.values.copy()
          z[~series.valid] = -np.inf
          # Allow plot-line timing tolerance without permitting picks inside guarded gaps.
          z = maximum_filter1d(z, size=2*int(CFG['pick_half_width_s']/STEP)+1, mode='constant', cval=-np.inf)
          z[~series.valid] = -np.inf
          return z


      def bank_stat(bank, cl, dg):
          idx = bank['pair_indices']
          scores = np.minimum(cl[bank['ic'][idx]], dg[bank['id'][idx]])
          j = int(np.argmax(scores))
          return float(scores[j]), int(idx[j])


      def correlation_for_bank(cl, dg, bank):
          # Conservative arrival-envelope screen; joint bank test below is physically paired.
          a, b = scan.Series(cl.time_epoch_s, cl.values, cl.valid.copy()), scan.Series(dg.time_epoch_s,dg.values,dg.valid.copy())
          for series, key in [(a,'cl'),(b,'dg')]:
              q = bank[key][bank['keep']]
              series.valid &= (series.time_epoch_s>=q.min()) & (series.time_epoch_s<=q.max())
          steps, response, count = scan.fft_correlation_response(a,b,STEP)
          lag = bank['dg'][bank['keep']]-bank['cl'][bank['keep']]
          valid = (steps*STEP>=lag.min())&(steps*STEP<=lag.max())&(count*STEP>=CFG['minimum_correlation_overlap_s'])&np.isfinite(response)
          if not valid.any():
              return float('nan'), float('nan'), steps, response, valid
          candidates = np.where(valid)[0]
          best = candidates[np.argmax(response[valid])]
          return float(response[best]), float(steps[best]*STEP), steps, response, valid


      def catalogue(traces):
          rows = list(csv.DictReader((DATA/'kadri-table1-transients.csv').open()))
          rows += [{'event_id':'figure_005204','time_utc':'00:52:04.350','bearing_deg':'57','source_status':'FIGURE1_MAJOR_BURST_REPICK; PAPER_APPROX_005200', 'figure9_status':'not_the_preferred_306_degree_event'},
                   {'event_id':'figure_003923','time_utc':'00:39:23.700','bearing_deg':'','source_status':'OUR_EXTRACTED_PEAK','figure9_status':'not_assigned_to_Table1_003902'},
                   {'event_id':'figure_005426','time_utc':'00:54:26.950','bearing_deg':'','source_status':'OUR_REPICK_NEAR_PREFERRED_EVENT','figure9_status':'not_independent_of_Table1_005430'},
                   {'event_id':'figure_003833','time_utc':'00:38:33.350','bearing_deg':'','source_status':'OUR_EXTRACTED_PEAK','figure9_status':'not_proven_identical_to_Table1_003829'}]
          lat, lon = dense_arc()
          az = base.initial_bearing_deg(lat,lon,STATIONS['CL'])
          out = []
          for row in rows:
              t = base.parse_utc('2014-03-08T'+row['time_utc']+'Z').timestamp()-MIDNIGHT
              p = next((p for p in 'abc' if traces[p].start_epoch_s-MIDNIGHT<=t<traces[p].start_epoch_s-MIDNIGHT+600),None)
              result = dict(row, arrival_s=t, panel=p)
              tr = traces[p]
              ts = tr.epoch_s-MIDNIGHT
              guarded = (ts>=ts[0]+10)&(ts<ts[0]+590)
              result['q99_peak_threshold'] = tr.threshold
              for width in [2,5,10]:
                  ix = np.where((np.abs(ts-t)<=width)&guarded)[0]
                  result[f'score_pm{width}s'] = float(tr.score[ix].max()) if len(ix) else None
              result['guarded'] = not (ts[0]+10 <= t < ts[0]+590)
              if row['bearing_deg']:
                  diff = (az-float(row['bearing_deg'])+180)%360-180
                  crossings = np.where((diff[:-1]*diff[1:]<=0)&(np.abs(np.diff(diff))<180))[0]
                  if len(crossings):
                      i = crossings[0]
                      la = float(np.interp(0,[diff[i],diff[i+1]][::1 if diff[i]<diff[i+1] else -1],lat[i:i+2][::1 if diff[i]<diff[i+1] else -1]))
                      lo = float(np.interp(la,lat,lon))
                      dcl, ddg = float(distance(la,lo,'CL')),float(distance(la,lo,'DG'))
                      result.update({'arc_latitude':la,'arc_longitude':lo,'CL_range_km':dcl,'DG_range_km':ddg,
                                     'impact_earliest_s':t-dcl/1.43,'impact_latest_s':t-dcl/1.57,
                                     'DG_earliest_s':t-dcl/1.43+ddg/1.57-5,'DG_latest_s':t-dcl/1.57+ddg/1.43+5})
              out.append(result)
          return out


      def controls(traces, residual, banks, cl, dg_raw, dg_filtered, events, rng):
          dg = {'unfiltered':dg_raw, 'filtered':dg_filtered}
          zs = {k:picked_scores(v) for k,v in dg.items()}
          zc = picked_scores(cl)
          result, null, correlations, nullcorr = {}, {}, {}, {}
          for label,b in banks.items():
              for method in dg:
                  key = label+' / '+method
                  score, i = bank_stat(b,zc,zs[method])
                  result[key] = {'joint_score':score,'sample_index':i,'source_latitude':float(b['lat'][i]),
                                 'source_longitude':float(b['lon'][i]),'source_time_s':float(b['t0'][i]),
                                 'cl_arrival_s':float(b['cl'][i]),'dg_arrival_s':float(b['dg'][i]),
                                 'CL_score':float(zc[b['ic'][i]]),'DG_score':float(zs[method][b['id'][i]]),
                                 'half_bank_score':float(np.max(np.minimum(zc[b['ic'][b['pair_indices'][:len(b['pair_indices'])//2]]],zs[method][b['id'][b['pair_indices'][:len(b['pair_indices'])//2]]])))}
                  r,lag,steps,resp,valid = correlation_for_bank(cl,dg[method],b)
                  result[key].update({'correlation_r':r,'correlation_lag_s':lag})
                  correlations[key] = (steps,resp,valid)
                  null[key], nullcorr[key] = [], []
          panel_null = {p:[] for p in 'abc'}
          score_errors = []
          blocknull = {k:[] for k in result}
          blockcorr = {k:[] for k in result}
          for rep in range(CFG['null_replicates']):
              scores = {}
              for p in 'abc':
                  pressure,error = aligned.iaaft_surrogate(traces[p].pressure_pa,rng,CFG['iaaft_iterations'])
                  s = base.score_signal(pressure,SCREEN)
                  scores[p] = s
                  panel_null[p].append(float(np.max(s[200:-200])))
                  score_errors.append(error)
              cs = score_series(traces,tuple('abc'),score_override=scores)
              z = picked_scores(cs)
              # Second null keeps actual short-time CL structure: permute 60 s blocks
              # within each panel, guarding block joins. It is a sensitivity control,
              # not an exchangeability claim for arbitrary non-stationary ocean data.
              bv, bm = cl.values.copy(),cl.valid.copy()
              for start in range(0,len(bv),int(600/STEP)):
                  nblock = int(60/STEP)
                  v = bv[start:start+int(600/STEP)].reshape(10,nblock)
                  m = bm[start:start+int(600/STEP)].reshape(10,nblock)
                  perm = rng.permutation(10)
                  vv,mm = v[perm].copy(),m[perm].copy()
                  mm[:,:int(4/STEP)] = False
                  mm[:,-int(4/STEP):] = False
                  bv[start:start+int(600/STEP)] = vv.ravel()
                  bm[start:start+int(600/STEP)] = mm.ravel()
              bs = scan.Series(cl.time_epoch_s,bv,bm)
              bz = picked_scores(bs)
              for label,b in banks.items():
                  for method in dg:
                      key = label+' / '+method
                      null[key].append(bank_stat(b,z,zs[method])[0])
                      blocknull[key].append(bank_stat(b,bz,zs[method])[0])
                      nullcorr[key].append(correlation_for_bank(cs,dg[method],b)[0])
                      blockcorr[key].append(correlation_for_bank(bs,dg[method],b)[0])
              if (rep+1)%50 == 0:
                  print(f'Null controls {rep+1}/{CFG["null_replicates"]}',flush=True)
          global_peaks = np.max(np.array(list(panel_null.values())),axis=0)
          for row in events:
              s = row['score_pm10s']
              row['CL_full_record_max_p'] = pvalue(global_peaks,s) if s is not None else None
              row['CL_panel_max_p'] = pvalue(panel_null[row['panel']],s) if s is not None else None
              for method, ds in dg.items():
                  if 'DG_earliest_s' in row:
                      valid = ds.valid&(ds.time_epoch_s>=row['DG_earliest_s'])&(ds.time_epoch_s<=row['DG_latest_s'])
                      row[f'DG_{method}_coverage_seconds'] = float(valid.sum()*STEP)
                      if valid.any():
                          indices = np.where(valid)[0];i = indices[np.argmax(ds.values[valid])]
                          row[f'DG_{method}_max_score'] = float(ds.values[i])
                          row[f'DG_{method}_pick_s'] = float(ds.time_epoch_s[i])
          for key,r in result.items():
              r['joint_iaaft_p'] = pvalue(null[key],r['joint_score'])
              r['joint_block_p'] = pvalue(blocknull[key],r['joint_score'])
              r['correlation_iaaft_p'] = pvalue(nullcorr[key],r['correlation_r'])
              r['correlation_block_p'] = pvalue(blockcorr[key],r['correlation_r'])
              # Selection over 3 spatial/flight hypotheses × 2 DG methods × 2 statistics.
              r['joint_iaaft_family_adjusted_p'] = min(1,12*r['joint_iaaft_p'])
              r['correlation_iaaft_family_adjusted_p'] = min(1,12*r['correlation_iaaft_p'])
              r['null_joint_q95'] = float(np.quantile(null[key],.95))
              r['null_correlation_q95'] = float(np.nanquantile(nullcorr[key],.95))
          stats = {'iaaft_spectrum_relative_error':quant(score_errors),'replicates':CFG['null_replicates'],
                   'resolution':1/(1+CFG['null_replicates']), 'CL_full_record_max_null_q95':float(np.quantile(global_peaks,.95))}
          np.savez_compressed(OUT/'null-controls.npz',**{k.replace(' / ','_'):v for k,v in null.items()},
                              **{'corr_'+k.replace(' / ','_'):v for k,v in nullcorr.items()},
                              **{'blocks_'+k.replace(' / ','_'):v for k,v in blocknull.items()},
                              **{'blockcorr_'+k.replace(' / ','_'):v for k,v in blockcorr.items()},
                              **{'CL_panel_'+k:v for k,v in panel_null.items()})
          return result,correlations,stats,global_peaks


      def checks():
          # Independently calculable time, units, energy and FFT fixtures.
          assert abs(60*40000/13800-173.91304347826087)<1e-9
          assert abs(13800*.3048/60-70.104)<1e-10
          assert abs(pressure_peak(1e5,2000)*2**(-1.13)-pressure_peak(1e5,4000))<1e-12
          assert abs(pressure_peak(8e5,2000)/pressure_peak(1e5,2000)-2**1.13)<1e-10
          rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
          x=rng.normal(size=300);y=np.roll(x,7)
          a=scan.Series(np.arange(300.),x,np.ones(300,bool));b=scan.Series(np.arange(300.),y,np.ones(300,bool))
          steps,r,n=scan.fft_correlation_response(a,b,1)
          i=np.where(steps==7)[0][0]
          assert abs(r[i]-np.corrcoef(x[:-7],y[7:])[0,1])<1e-10
          s,e=aligned.iaaft_surrogate(x,rng,30)
          assert np.array_equal(np.sort(x),np.sort(s))
          # Guarded high spikes never become searchable picks.
          a.values[0]=1000;a.valid[:10]=False
          assert not np.isfinite(picked_scores(a)[0])
          return ['constant-rate maximum fall time','feet/minute conversion','pressure range scaling',
                  'energy cube-root scaling','FFT versus scalar correlation','IAAFT marginal preservation','guarded-boundary exclusion']


      def main():
          started=time.perf_counter();OUT.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
          rng=np.random.default_rng(CFG['seed'])
          passed=checks();traces=base.load_traces(BASECFG)
          chosen,filter_rows,residual,periods=filter_control(traces,rng)
          print('Selected held-out projection rank',chosen,flush=True)
          grids,gridpath=drift_grids()
          cl=score_series(traces,tuple('abc'));dg_raw=score_series(traces,('d','e'))
          dg_filtered=score_series(traces,('d','e'),residual)
          banks,summary,intervals,energy=mc_banks(grids,rng,cl,dg_raw)
          print('Holland impact quantiles',[utc(x) for x in intervals['impact_time_s']],flush=True)
          print('Coverage',json.dumps(summary),flush=True)
          events=catalogue(traces)
          result,correlations,nullstats,global_peaks=controls(traces,residual,banks,cl,dg_raw,dg_filtered,events,rng)
          save_csv('event-tests.csv',events)
          save_csv('hypothesis-tests.csv',[dict(hypothesis=k,**v) for k,v in result.items()])
          save_csv('model-ranges-and-coverage.csv',[dict(hypothesis=k,**v) for k,v in summary.items()])
          np.savez_compressed(OUT/'plot-arrays.npz',cl_time=cl.time_epoch_s,cl_score=cl.values,cl_valid=cl.valid,
                              dg_time=dg_raw.time_epoch_s,dg_raw_score=dg_raw.values,dg_filtered_score=dg_filtered.values,
                              dg_valid=dg_raw.valid,**{p+'_pressure':traces[p].pressure_pa for p in traces},
                              **{p+'_residual':v for p,v in residual.items()},
                              **{k.replace(' / ','_')+'_lag':v[0]*STEP for k,v in correlations.items()},
                              **{k.replace(' / ','_')+'_corr':v[1] for k,v in correlations.items()},
                              **{k.replace(' / ','_')+'_valid':v[2] for k,v in correlations.items()})
          inputs=[CFG_PATH,Path(__file__),base.CONFIG_PATH,base.METADATA_PATH,base.ARC_PATH,
                  DATA/'filter-sensitivity-config.json',DATA/'kadri-table1-transients.csv',gridpath]
          inputs+=list((DATA/'kadri-figure-extraction/figure9-vector-traces').glob('*.csv'))
          inputs+=[Path(m.__file__) for m in [base,airgun,overview,aligned,scan]]
          payload={'configuration':CFG,'status':'PUBLICATION_TRACE_CONDITIONAL_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY',
                   'holland':intervals,'hypotheses':summary,'tests':result,'filter_rank':chosen,'filter_rows':filter_rows,
                   'airgun_periods_s':periods,'null_controls':nullstats,'checks_passed':passed,
                   'input_sha256':{str(p.resolve()):hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in inputs},
                   'wall_seconds':time.perf_counter()-started,'maximum_rss_mib':resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss/1024}
          (OUT/'results.json').write_text(json.dumps(payload,indent=2))
          print(json.dumps({'tests':result,'wall_seconds':payload['wall_seconds']},indent=2),flush=True)


      if __name__=='__main__':
          main()

  ── Assistant
  The first run shows why the control matters: the strongest timing-compatible pairs reuse the prominent CL burst near **00:52:04**. They look unusual against phase-randomised noise, but not when the control preserves actual bursts and moves them between time blocks.

  Kadri reports a bearing of about **57°** for that burst, inconsistent with these southern-ocean source hypotheses if it is the same event. I’m repeating the tests with it excluded before drawing conclusions.

      # Methods and assumptions: conditional hydroacoustic tests

      This is an exploratory test of **compatibility**, using material already selected and published by Kadri. It is not an independent replication of his array analysis, an aircraft detection, a calibrated non-detection, or an update to the MH370 posterior. All dates are 8 March 2014 and all clocks are UTC. British English is used throughout.

      ## 1. What is measured, and what is assumed?

      **Available representation.** We use the pressure polylines extracted from the vector drawings in Figure 9a–e of [Kadri (2024)](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41598-024-60529-1). CL covers 00:27–00:57; DG covers 01:00–01:20. Each ten-minute panel has about 9,000 plotted vertices, not a recoverable uniformly sampled hydrophone record. The published processing includes high-pass and band-pass filtering; its complete per-panel transfer function and plotting decimation are unknown. The 0.05 s interpolation grid is computational convenience, **not** 20 Hz recovered information. The plot-line thickness alone corresponds to roughly half a second horizontally. Millisecond strings inherited from earlier peak picks are identifiers, not justified accuracy.

      Ten seconds at each panel boundary are excluded. This explicitly removes the DG feature around 01:10:00.5 that previously appeared aligned with a CL peak. We cannot recover the three hydrophone channels, source bearing, propagation phase, calibrated spectrum, or a station detection probability from these drawings. The vertical scale is pressure in pascals (Pa); a plot-derived pressure statistic is not total received acoustic energy.

      **Spatial alternatives.** BRAN2016, OSCAR v2 Final and GLORYS12/WAVERYS use the existing checksum-recorded Pléiades transport-compatibility surfaces separately. These are conditional on an exactly-one-of-twelve object-identity mixture, 10 km endpoint kernels, the declared windage mixture and 5 NM/day dispersion. They are **not** independently established impact PDFs. We sample each surface inside its own 95% highest-density region, renormalising the retained cell masses. The transport calculation starts at 00:19; using the same surface for impacts up to thirty minutes later neglects the resulting sub-hour drift correction. That approximation is explicit; the transport integration is not rerun here. The three surfaces are not multiplied together. The current integrated flight/BTO/BFO posterior is not an input to this stand-alone test.

      For these Pléiades tests, source time is uniform from 00:19:37.443 to 00:49:37.443. This thirty-minute control is not a fitted glide-time posterior. For the unarrested-descent test, the starting point is uniform in arclength along the FL400 seventh-arc reference between 20°S and 45°S. A uniformly sampled horizontal speed, 0–250 m/s, and direction produce displacement during descent. The reference arc is not corrected for the sampled altitude or refitted to BTO; this is a coarse spatial control, not a dynamically feasible full-flight ensemble.

      ## 2. Unarrested descent and working coupling

      [Holland (2018), Table VII](https://arxiv.org/abs/1702.02432v3), supplies a conditional range of 13,800–25,300 ft/min at 00:19:37. It does not supply a 95% distribution. We assume altitude uniformly distributed over 20,000–40,000 ft and a triangular downward-speed density with its mode at 19,550 ft/min. Speed continues unchanged to contact: **no recovery, levelling-off or phugoid**. This is neither an aerodynamic acceleration simulation nor an assertion that the aircraft was at that altitude. Uniform-rate and lower-altitude controls are also tabulated.

      \[
      \Delta t=\frac{h}{w},\qquad t_I=t_{19}+\Delta t,\qquad
      E_n=\frac12 m w^2,\qquad E_k=\frac12m(w^2+v_h^2).
      \]

      Here height and speed use compatible SI units. The mass, 174,369 kg, is 9M-MRO's documented final-loadsheet **zero-fuel weight**, used as an impact-mass surrogate, not a measured 00:19 mass. Mechanical potential energy is not added again at contact: the fixed-speed descent assumes ongoing aerodynamic dissipation. Horizontal energy is reported but is not included in the primary coupling proxy.

      Our agreed working hypothesis is

      \[
      \log_{10}\eta\sim\mathcal U(-6,-4),\qquad E_c=\eta E_n.
      \]

      Thus the assumed effective coupling is 0.0001%–0.01% of normal kinetic energy, with equal weight per decade. Its geometric midpoint is 0.001%. The upper end is motivated by the order-of-magnitude surface-to-SOFAR estimate discussed for the F-35 impact by [Brown et al. (2026), §3.2](https://arxiv.org/html/2604.12723v1#S3.SS2). That paper explicitly warns that its relatively shallow sound channel could make the example unusually efficient. The lower end and the distribution are **our working choices**, not aircraft-impact statistics. A much wider log-uniform control, 10⁻⁸–10⁻², tests dependence on this choice. The effective energy is best regarded as SOFAR-equivalent source energy, not a directly measured energy budget.

      For continuity with the earlier simulations, our primary pressure-scale control is Brown et al.'s equation (2):

      \[
      W=\frac{E_c}{4.184\times10^6}\ {\rm kg\ TNT},\qquad
      p_{\rm pk}(R,L)=52.4\times10^6
      \left(\frac{R}{W^{1/3}}\right)^{-1.13}10^{-L/20}\ {\rm Pa},
      \]

      with range \(R\) in metres. It is an **explosion-equivalent extrapolation**, not a validated aircraft-to-CL/DG propagation model. Brown et al. also state that this equation could not be fully validated for modern SOFAR explosions. Additional loss \(L=0,10,20,30\) dB is shown as separate alternatives. Zero *additional* loss is the optimistic reference; it is not zero spreading loss. None of these cases models the actual bathymetric path, frequency-dependent loss, source radiation pattern or station response. They are not mixed with invented probabilities.

      As independent energy bookkeeping, we also tabulate an ideal cylindrical-duct control:

      \[
      F=\frac{f_bE_c10^{-L/10}}{2\pi RH},\quad
      S_p=\int p^2dt=\rho cF,\quad
      p_{\rm rms}=\sqrt{S_p/T}.
      \]

      This assumes a travelling wave, azimuthally uniform energy, duct height \(H=1,000\) m, a retained-band fraction \(f_b=0.1\), \(\rho=1,025\) kg/m³, local \(c=1,500\) m/s and a two-second packet \(T\). \(F\) is energy per unit area (J/m²), not total energy received by a sensor; \(S_p\) is pressure exposure (Pa² s). These are analyst-selected bookkeeping controls, not a second calibrated prediction. Changing \(f_b,H,T\) rescales RMS by \(\sqrt{f_b/(HT)}\); they must not be used to convert the explosion proxy's peak directly into RMS. Neither model supplies an acoustic–gravity Green function or supports a calibrated claim of non-detection.

      All quoted 95% intervals are the **2.5th–97.5th percentiles conditional on these assumptions**. They are not empirical confidence bounds on MH370's actual state or coupling.

      ## 3. Propagation and the joint search

      Each station gets its own independent effective celerity, uniform over 1.43–1.57 km/s, following the numerical range used by Kadri on p. 13. An independent ±5 s timing allowance is added at each station:

      \[
      t_s=t_I+\frac{R_s(\mathbf x)}{c_s}+\epsilon_s,\qquad
      \tau=t_{DG}-t_{CL}.
      \]

      The separate celerities allow differential path delays of minutes; the ±5 s term is **not the full propagation uncertainty**. It is a short residual timing allowance inspired by our previous homogeneous-layer acoustic–gravity control, not a new AGW simulation. Multipath, blockage and strongly dispersive modes remain unmodelled. A source-compatible pair is not unique: many source positions and celerities produce the same arrival-time difference.

      We draw 250,000 source/timing samples per family. The Holland search retains the central 95% of its sampled impact times; Pléiades uses the stated thirty-minute interval. Draws whose receptions lie outside the guarded drawings are counted as unavailable, not as non-detections. The bank statistic is the maximum of the weaker of the two stations' local scores, allowing ±2 s around each predicted arrival:

      \[
      J=\max_{\theta\in\Theta_{\rm covered}}
      \min\{\max_{|u|\le2s} Z_{CL}(t_{CL}(\theta)+u),
             \max_{|u|\le2s} Z_{DG}(t_{DG}(\theta)+u)\}.
      \]

      This is a deliberately simple **joint excess-amplitude screen**, not a matched filter for a known aircraft waveform. We also scan Pearson correlation of the two local-score series across all half-second lags admitted by each family's sampled arrival envelope, requiring at least 120 s of valid overlap. The latter envelope test is deliberately looser than the physically paired sample bank: a high whole-record correlation alone need not correspond to one feasible impact time. No pressure-phase correlation is interpreted as physical waveform coherence.

      The source-bank maximum is checked against its first-half maximum. This checks stability of the reported maximum, not convergence of a source posterior: a maximum shared by many samples does not localise the source.

      ## 4. Local-energy score and DG filtering

      The name *local-energy score* is shorthand for our robust, dimensionless RMS-excess statistic:

      \[
      R_T(t)=\sqrt{T^{-1}\int_{t-T/2}^{t+T/2}p(u)^2du},\qquad
      Z(t)=\max_{T\in\{1,2,4\}\,s}
      \frac{R_T(t)-\operatorname{median}(R_T)}
      {1.4826\operatorname{median}|R_T-\operatorname{median}(R_T)|}.
      \]

      Baselines are computed separately on each panel's guarded interior. RMS/short-time power is standard signal processing; this exact normalisation and maximum-over-three-windows are our exploratory detector. A score of five is **not five Gaussian standard deviations**, and its local 99th-percentile peak threshold is not a 1% experiment-wide false-alarm probability.

      DG pulse timing is estimated separately in its two panels. For each complete nine-second pulse neighbourhood we train a median-pulse/PCA subspace on the other cycles, excluding the target and its neighbours. The current test uses unaligned cycles so no all-cycle alignment template leaks the target into training. Ranks 0, 1, 2, 4, 8, 12 and 20 are compared. Rank zero leaves the trace unchanged. Subtraction is the orthogonal projection

      \[
      \mathbf r_k=(I-Q_{-k}Q_{-k}^{\mathsf T})\mathbf p_k.
      \]

      Rank is chosen for maximum median RMS suppression **subject to** at least 90% median and 75% lower-decile matched-amplitude retention in both panels. The retention controls insert 45 unit-norm Gaussian/cosine pulse shapes per cycle (width parameters 0.2, 0.5 and 1 s; offsets −3, −1, 0, 1 and 3 s; frequencies 0, 1 and 3 Hz). These probe the plotted representation; they are not the original 2–40 Hz aircraft signals. With the held-out subspace fixed, the recovered matched amplitude is \(1-\|Q^\mathsf Ts\|^2\). This conditional linear-response test does not establish real-aircraft detection efficiency or test how injected signals would perturb the shot-time estimator. Training is independent of the **CL match**, but background ocean events may still contaminate the DG training cycles. No beamforming or AIS-driven shot reconstruction is possible from the single plotted DG series alone.

      ## 5. Chance coincidence, bearings and the catalogue

      We use 399 controls per test under two deliberately different nulls. IAAFT randomises CL pressure while preserving its amplitude distribution and approximately its Fourier magnitudes; local scores are recomputed. DG, including all its remaining airgun structure, stays fixed. We record spectral mismatch rather than assuming exact preservation. See [Schreiber and Schmitz (2000), §§4.3–4.6 and Appendix A.2](https://arxiv.org/html/chao-dyn/9909037v1).

      A second control permutes sixty-second blocks within each CL panel, preserving real short-time bursts; four seconds around artificial joins are excluded. This addresses the practically important question: *does an alignment remain unusual when real ocean bursts, rather than only phase-randomised noise, are available to align?* Block exchangeability and within-panel stationarity remain assumptions. Disagreement between nulls is reported, not resolved by choosing the smaller p-value.

      \[
      \widehat p=\frac{1+\sum_{b=1}^{B}{\bf1}(S_b\ge S_{\rm obs})}{B+1}.
      \]

      The finest p-value resolution is 1/400, not zero. Searches are repeated over their entire allowed domains for every control. A Bonferroni correction covers the 32 current searches: four source families × two DG treatments × two statistics × two bearing treatments. This does **not** retrospectively correct every analysis ever explored in this project or Kadri's original event selection. Catalogue CL p-values separately use the maximum over all three available CL panels and all score windows; they test presence of an unusual plotted burst, not aircraft identity.

      The second bearing treatment excludes 00:51:45–00:52:20 and 00:54:10–00:54:50 at CL. This is conditional on assigning the published 57° and 306.18° bearings to these prominent bursts. The first points away from our southern arc; the preferred second bearing implies much later impact near its own arc intersection than the unarrested scenario, and does not cross the Pléiades support. The veto is not a new bearing measurement. Other faint catalogue bearings are evaluated separately because their exact linkage to plotted excursions is unresolved.

      All nineteen Table 1 rows, plus four explicitly labelled project re-picks, are audited in ±2, ±5 and ±10 s windows. A window extending into an excluded panel edge is marked accordingly. Re-picks near the same burst are not independent events. Where a reported CL bearing intersects the 20°S–45°S FL400 arc, we calculate impact and DG reception envelopes by range/celerity, **conditional on exact bearing and an impact on that reference arc**. These are endpoint envelopes, not 95% intervals. Off-arc impacts and bearing uncertainties would broaden or move them. Finding the largest DG excursion inside a wide predicted window is not verification; repeated airguns offer many chances for that to happen. A lack of a peak in a selected drawing is not proof that Kadri did not measure a transient in the raw array.

      ## 6. What would allow estimator integration?

      This run leaves the estimator unchanged. An acoustic likelihood requires calibrated raw channels, timing and responses; an explicit background/transient/airgun model; source-to-array propagation families; a predeclared detector; injection–recovery by source state; and correction for candidate and search selection. Non-detection then enters through an appropriate \(1-P_D(\theta)\), conditioned on actual data coverage—not through the absence of a conspicuous PDF peak. The present outputs support prioritising conditional tests for Kadri, not posterior weights.

      ## Located source ledger

      | Claim used | Retrieved source and locator | Check |
      |---|---|---|
      | High-rate bounds at the later final transmission | Holland, arXiv:1702.02432v3, PDF p. 10, Table VII: 13,800–25,300 ft/min | FOUND in local full PDF; distributions are our assumptions |
      | Aircraft mass surrogate | Malaysian Safety Investigation Report (2018), PDF p. 97 / printed p. 51, Table 1.6D: zero-fuel weight 174,369 kg | FOUND in local full PDF; not impact-mass measurement |
      | Prior filtering and three-element array | Kadri (2024), PDF p. 11, “Signal processing and bearing calculation” | FOUND; complete plot transfer function unavailable |
      | Celerity and preferred candidate | Kadri, PDF pp. 13–14, Table 1 and caption | FOUND; 00:54 body rounding is not substituted for 00:54:30 table time |
      | F-35 coupling is a limited analogue | Brown et al., arXiv:2604.12723v1, §3.2, paragraphs beginning “For the F-35 crash” and “Note, however” | FOUND in retrieved full HTML; this source explicitly cautions about unusually efficient shallow-channel coupling |
      | Explosion pressure proxy and validation caveat | Brown et al., §2, Eq. (2) and immediately following discussion | FOUND; extrapolation retained as a working scale, not a calibrated prediction |
      | Iterative surrogate construction and endpoint caveats | Schreiber & Schmitz, arXiv:chao-dyn/9909037v1, §§4.3–4.6 and Appendix A.2 | FOUND in retrieved full HTML; null suitability remains conditional |

      The seed, numerical configuration, input/code SHA-256 identities, runtime, memory and focused checks are recorded in `results.json`. Output quantiles, p-values, event times and plots are generated from the run artifacts, not hand-edited.

      #!/usr/bin/env python3
      """Render the conditional-test artifacts as inspected PNG/SVG and HTML/Markdown."""
      from __future__ import annotations
      import csv
      import hashlib
      import html
      import json
      import re
      import zipfile
      from pathlib import Path

      import matplotlib
      matplotlib.use('Agg')
      import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
      from matplotlib.ticker import FuncFormatter
      import numpy as np
      from markdown_it import MarkdownIt

      import conditional_hypothesis_tests as c

      OUT=c.OUT
      P,R,G=c.PURPLE,c.RED,c.GREY
      FIGURES=[]
      CHECKS=[]
      plt.rcParams.update({'font.family':'DejaVu Sans','font.size':12,'axes.titlesize':13,
                           'axes.labelsize':12,'svg.fonttype':'none','svg.hashsalt':'conditional-hydro-tests',
                           'axes.spines.top':False,'axes.spines.right':False})


      def readcsv(name):
          return list(csv.DictReader((OUT/name).open()))


      def number(value):
          return float(value) if value not in [None,''] else float('nan')


      def times(q):
          return f'{c.utc(q[0])}–{c.utc(q[-1])}'


      def clock_axis(ax):
          ax.xaxis.set_major_formatter(FuncFormatter(lambda x,_:c.utc(x*60)))
          ax.tick_params(axis='x',labelsize=10)


      def finish(fig,name,title,caption):
          fig.suptitle(title,fontsize=18,y=.98)
          fig.tight_layout(rect=(0,.07,1,.925),h_pad=2.4,w_pad=2.5)
          fig.text(.025,.018,caption,fontsize=10,va='bottom',ha='left')
          fig.canvas.draw()
          renderer=fig.canvas.get_renderer()
          # Check every visible text bounding box against the actual canvas.
          from matplotlib.text import Text
          outside=[]
          for artist in fig.findobj(match=Text):
              if artist.get_visible() and artist.get_text():
                  b=artist.get_window_extent(renderer)
                  if b.width and b.height and (b.x0 < -2 or b.y0 < -2 or b.x1>fig.bbox.width+2 or b.y1>fig.bbox.height+2):
                      outside.append(artist.get_text())
          CHECKS.append({'figure':name,'text_outside_canvas':outside})
          if outside:raise ValueError(f'{name}: text outside canvas {outside}')
          fig.savefig(OUT/(name+'.png'),dpi=170)
          fig.savefig(OUT/(name+'.svg'))
          plt.close(fig)
          FIGURES.append((name,title,caption))


      def coverage_plot(d):
          fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(13,6.9))
          names=list(d['hypotheses'])
          for i,label in enumerate(names):
              row=d['hypotheses'][label]
              for dy,st,col in [(-.12,'cl',P),(.12,'dg',R)]:
                  q=np.array(row[st+'_arrival_s'])/60
                  ax.plot(q[[0,2]],[i+dy]*2,color=col,lw=6,solid_capstyle='butt')
                  ax.plot(q[1],i+dy,'o',color='white',mec=col,mew=2)
              ax.text(94,i,f"{row['joint_coverage_fraction']:.0%}",va='center',ha='center',fontsize=13)
          for lo,hi,col,label in [(27,57,P,'CL drawing coverage'),(60,80,R,'DG drawing coverage')]:
              ax.plot([lo,hi],[-.8,-.8],lw=8,color=col,label=label)
          ax.set(yticks=range(len(names)),yticklabels=names,xlim=(25,98),ylim=(len(names)-.5,-1.2),xlabel='UTC reception time (8 March 2014)')
          ax.text(94,-.55,'Both stations\ncovered',ha='center',va='bottom',fontsize=10)
          ax.legend(loc='lower left',fontsize=11)
          clock_axis(ax);ax.grid(axis='x',alpha=.15)
          finish(fig,'arrival-windows-and-coverage','Predicted receptions versus the drawings we actually have',
                 'Bars: central 95% model-conditional reception intervals; white dots: medians. Purple: CL; red: DG.\nCoverage fractions count each paired Monte Carlo draw; ten-second panel-edge guards are excluded.')


      def energy_plot(d,rows):
          fig,axes=plt.subplots(2,2,figsize=(13,9))
          h=d['holland']
          ax=axes[0,0]
          t=np.array(h['impact_delay_s'])
          ax.plot(t[[0,2]],[0,0],color=P,lw=9);ax.plot(t[1],0,'o',mfc='white',mec=P,ms=10)
          ax.plot(h['hard_delay_s'],[-.22,-.22],color=G,lw=4)
          ax.set(xlim=(0,185),ylim=(-.55,.65),yticks=[],xlabel='Seconds after 00:19:37.443',title='Unarrested descent: conditional time to impact')
          ax.text(.04,.83,f"95%: {t[0]:.0f}–{t[2]:.0f} s  |  median {t[1]:.0f} s\nImpact: {times(h['impact_time_s'])} UTC",transform=ax.transAxes,va='top',fontsize=12)
          ax.text(.04,.09,'Grey: full 20,000–40,000 ft / rate-bound envelope',transform=ax.transAxes,fontsize=10)
          ax=axes[0,1]
          for i,(key,label,col) in enumerate([('normal_energy_j','Normal kinetic',P),('total_kinetic_energy_j','Total kinetic',G),('coupled_energy_j','Effective coupled',R)]):
              q=np.array(h[key]);ax.plot(q[[0,2]],[i,i],color=col,lw=7);ax.plot(q[1],i,'o',mfc='white',mec=col)
          ax.set(xscale='log',yticks=[0,1,2],yticklabels=['Normal kinetic','Total kinetic','Effective coupled'],xlabel='Energy (J)',title='Energy range under the working assumptions')
          ax.grid(axis='x',alpha=.15)
          for ax,station in zip(axes[1],['CL','DG']):
              for prior,dy,col in [('working',-.09,P),('broad',.09,G)]:
                  rr=[r for r in rows if r['station']==station and r['coupling_prior']==prior]
                  for r in rr:
                      loss=number(r['excess_loss_db']);i=loss/10
                      q=[number(r[k]) for k in ['peak_q025_pa','peak_median_pa','peak_q975_pa']]
                      ax.plot([q[0],q[2]],[i+dy]*2,lw=5,color=col,label=prior if i==0 else None)
                      ax.plot(q[1],i+dy,'o',mfc='white',mec=col)
              ax.set(xscale='log',yticks=range(4),yticklabels=['0','10','20','30'],xlabel='Predicted peak pressure (Pa)',ylabel='Additional loss (dB)',title=station+' — explosion-equivalent pressure scale')
              ax.invert_yaxis();ax.grid(axis='x',alpha=.15);ax.legend(fontsize=10)
          finish(fig,'holland-timing-energy-pressure','Unarrested Holland descent: a declared working hypothesis',
                 'Working coupling: log-uniform 10⁻⁶–10⁻⁴ of normal kinetic energy; broad control: 10⁻⁸–10⁻².\n95% bars are prior-conditional, not empirical confidence bounds. Pressure is an unvalidated explosion-equivalent extrapolation.')


      def filtering_plot(d,a):
          fig,axes=plt.subplots(2,2,figsize=(13,9))
          for ax,panel in zip(axes[0],['d','e']):
              rr=[r for r in d['filter_rows'] if r['panel']==panel]
              for y,key,col,label in [('','median_rms_removed_fraction',G,'RMS removed'),('','median_matched_amplitude_retention',P,'Median amplitude retained'),('','q10_matched_amplitude_retention',R,'Lower decile retained')]:
                  ax.plot([r['rank'] for r in rr],[100*r[key] for r in rr],'-o',color=col,label=label)
              ax.axvline(d['filter_rank'],color=P,ls=':',lw=2)
              ax.set(xlabel='Subspace rank (0 = unchanged)',ylabel='Per cent',ylim=(-3,106),title=f"DG panel {panel} — fitted period {d['airgun_periods_s'][panel]:.3f} s")
              ax.legend(fontsize=9,loc='lower right');ax.grid(alpha=.13)
          for ax,panel,lo,hi in [(axes[1,0],'d',180,210),(axes[1,1],'e',155,185)]:
              t=np.arange(len(a[panel+'_pressure']))*c.DT
              use=(t>=lo)&(t<=hi)
              ax.plot(t[use],a[panel+'_pressure'][use],color=G,lw=1,label='Published trace')
              ax.plot(t[use],a[panel+'_residual'][use],color=P,lw=1,label='Selected filter')
              offset=3600 if panel=='d' else 4200
              ax.xaxis.set_major_formatter(FuncFormatter(lambda x,_,off=offset:c.utc(x+off)))
              ax.set(xlabel='UTC reception time',ylabel='Pressure (Pa)',title='Airgun train and larger excursion — '+('01:03 region' if panel=='d' else '01:12 region'))
              ax.tick_params(axis='x',labelsize=10);ax.legend(fontsize=10)
          finish(fig,'airgun-filter-trade-off','DG suppression versus preservation of possible additional signals',
                 'Selected rank is chosen without consulting CL matches. Stronger suppression removes more injected pulse shapes.\nInjected shapes test the plotted representation only; the selected filter does not establish that remaining peaks are independent sources.')


      def traces_plot(d,a):
          fig,axes=plt.subplots(2,1,figsize=(14,9))
          for ax,st,tk,zk in [(axes[0],'CL','cl_time','cl_score'),(axes[1],'DG','dg_time','dg_filtered_score')]:
              valid=a['cl_valid'] if st=='CL' else a['dg_valid']
              z=a[zk].copy();z[~valid]=np.nan
              if st=='DG':
                  zz=a['dg_raw_score'].copy();zz[~valid]=np.nan
                  ax.plot(a[tk]/60,zz,color=G,lw=.75,label='Unfiltered')
              ax.plot(a[tk]/60,z,color=P,lw=1.2,label='CL score' if st=='CL' else 'Selected filter')
              ax.set(ylabel='RMS-excess score Z',xlabel='UTC reception time',title=st+' — full available record, with panel-edge guards')
              clock_axis(ax);ax.grid(alpha=.15);ax.legend(loc='upper left')
              if st=='CL':
                  ax.axhline(d['null_controls']['CL_full_record_max_null_q95'],color=R,ls='--',lw=1.5,label='Full-record IAAFT 95% maximum')
                  ax.axvspan(51.75,52+20/60,color=R,alpha=.12)
                  ax.axvspan(54+10/60,54+50/60,color=R,alpha=.12)
                  ax.legend(fontsize=9,loc='upper left')
          finish(fig,'full-record-local-energy','Local-energy screens: what the publication traces show',
                 'Z = max over 1, 2 and 4 s RMS windows of (RMS − panel median)/(1.4826 × panel MAD). Z is not a Gaussian sigma.\nCL dashed line: phase-randomised full-record maximum control. Shading: the two major-burst windows excluded in the bearing-veto sensitivity.')


      def map_plot(d,events):
          grids,_=c.drift_grids()
          fig,axes=plt.subplots(1,2,figsize=(14,8))
          ax=axes[0];xy=c.arc();ax.plot(xy[:,0],xy[:,1],color='#41434a',lw=1.5,label='FL400 seventh-arc reference')
          selected=['00:39:02','00:49:58','00:53:31','00:54:30']
          for i,event in enumerate([e for e in events if e['time_utc'] in selected]):
              lo,la=number(event.get('arc_longitude')),number(event.get('arc_latitude'))
              ax.plot([114.141,lo],[-34.892,la],lw=1,color=[G,P,R,'#855289'][i],label=event['time_utc']+' / '+event['bearing_deg']+'°')
              ax.plot(lo,la,'o',color=[G,P,R,'#855289'][i]);ax.annotate(str(i+1),(lo,la),xytext=(5,6),textcoords='offset points',fontsize=12)
          ax.plot(114.141,-34.892,'^',color='black',ms=9);ax.text(113.5,-35.8,'CL',ha='right')
          ax.set(xlim=(78,116),ylim=(-44,-20),xlabel='Longitude (°E)',ylabel='Latitude (°)',title='Reported bearings: conditional arc intersections')
          ax.legend(fontsize=10,loc='lower left');ax.grid(alpha=.15)
          ax=axes[1];ax.plot(xy[:,0],xy[:,1],color='#41434a',lw=1.5)
          for (label,g),col,style in zip(grids.items(),[P,R,'#6d7077'],['solid','dashed','dotted']):
              density=g['densityPerKm2'];order=np.argsort(density)[::-1];cum=np.cumsum(g['probabilityMass'][order])
              levels=sorted([density[order[np.searchsorted(cum,q)]] for q in [.5,.95]])
              ax.tricontour(g['longitude'],g['latitude'],density,levels=levels,colors=[col],linewidths=[1.2,2.3],linestyles=style)
              ax.plot([],[],color=col,ls=style,label=label+' 50% / 95%')
          for event in events:
              if event['time_utc'] in ['00:49:58','00:53:31']:
                  lo,la=number(event['arc_longitude']),number(event['arc_latitude'])
                  ax.plot(lo,la,'x',color='black',ms=9,mew=2)
                  ax.annotate(event['time_utc'],(lo,la),xytext=(10,-15),textcoords='offset points',fontsize=11)
          ax.set(xlim=(88.5,95),ylim=(-38,-33),xlabel='Longitude (°E)',ylabel='Latitude (°)',title='Pléiades transport conditionals — separate families')
          ax.legend(fontsize=10,loc='upper right');ax.grid(alpha=.15)
          finish(fig,'catalogue-bearings-and-pleiades','Geometry compatibility is not signal identification',
                 'Contours are the existing conditional transport densities, not the integrated flight/BTO/BFO posterior.\nBearings are imported from Kadri; we cannot remeasure them from the single plotted trace. Exact-arc intersections neglect off-arc flight.')


      def alignment_plot(d,a,label):
          grids,_=c.drift_grids();g=grids[label];i=g['mode'];la,lo=g['latitude'][i],g['longitude'][i]
          shiftcl=float(c.distance(la,lo,'CL'))/1.5;shiftdg=float(c.distance(la,lo,'DG'))/1.5
          fig,axes=plt.subplots(2,1,figsize=(13,8),sharex=True)
          for ax,st,tk,zk,shift in [(axes[0],'CL','cl_time','cl_score',shiftcl),(axes[1],'DG','dg_time','dg_filtered_score',shiftdg)]:
              valid=a['cl_valid'] if st=='CL' else a['dg_valid'];z=a[zk].copy();z[~valid]=np.nan
              if st=='DG':
                  raw=a['dg_raw_score'].copy();raw[~valid]=np.nan
                  ax.plot((a[tk]-shift)/60,raw,color=G,lw=.7,label='Without additional filtering')
              ax.plot((a[tk]-shift)/60,z,color=P,lw=1.1,label='CL score' if st=='CL' else 'Selected DG filter')
              ax.axvspan(c.TREF/60,(c.TREF+1800)/60,color=P,alpha=.04)
              ax.set(ylabel='RMS-excess score Z',title=f'{st}: central travel time {shift/60:.2f} min')
              ax.legend(loc='upper left',fontsize=10);ax.grid(alpha=.15)
          axes[-1].set(xlabel='Implied source UTC at the chosen location and 1.50 km/s',xlim=(19,40));clock_axis(axes[-1])
          finish(fig,'source-alignment-'+label.lower(),f'{label} mode: {abs(la):.3f}°S, {lo:.3f}°E — central alignment only',
                 'Grey DG retains the periodic interference; purple is the preservation-constrained filter. Gaps stay gaps.\nThe tests vary source location, each path celerity and residual timing. This fixed-speed display is not a recovered common event.')


      def pair_plot(d,a,label):
          key=label+' / filtered / major-burst bearing veto';r=d['tests'][key]
          fig,axes=plt.subplots(2,1,figsize=(12,7.5),sharex=True)
          for ax,st,tk,zk,pred in [(axes[0],'CL','cl_time','cl_score',r['cl_arrival_s']),(axes[1],'DG','dg_time','dg_filtered_score',r['dg_arrival_s'])]:
              valid=a['cl_valid'] if st=='CL' else a['dg_valid'];use=valid&(np.abs(a[tk]-pred)<55)
              if st=='DG':ax.plot(a[tk][use]-pred,a['dg_raw_score'][use],color=G,lw=1,label='Unfiltered DG')
              ax.plot(a[tk][use]-pred,a[zk][use],color=P,lw=1.7,label=st+' score')
              ax.axvspan(-2,2,color=R,alpha=.12)
              ax.set(ylabel='RMS-excess score Z',title=f"{st}: predicted arrival ≈{c.utc(pred)} UTC; selected score peak ≈{c.utc(r[st+'_actual_score_peak_s'])}")
              ax.grid(alpha=.15);ax.legend(fontsize=10)
          axes[-1].set(xlabel='Seconds relative to each station’s predicted arrival',xlim=(-55,55))
          finish(fig,'best-surviving-pair-'+label.lower(),f'{label}: strongest timing-compatible pair after major-burst veto',
                 f"One compatible draw: {abs(r['source_latitude']):.2f}°S, {r['source_longitude']:.2f}°E; impact ≈{c.utc(r['source_time_s'])} UTC. Not a location estimate.\nJoint-score p: IAAFT {r['joint_iaaft_p']:.3f}; burst-preserving blocks {r['joint_block_p']:.3f}. Other catalogue bearings still require verification.")


      def correlation_plot(d,a):
          fig,axes=plt.subplots(4,2,figsize=(14,13))
          for i,label in enumerate(d['hypotheses']):
              for j,(prefix,suffix,title) in enumerate([('','','All usable times'),('excluded_',' / major-burst bearing veto','Major-burst bearing veto')]):
                  ax=axes[i,j]
                  for method,col in [('unfiltered',G),('filtered',P)]:
                      arrkey=prefix+label+'_'+method
                      valid=a[arrkey+'_valid'];z=a[arrkey+'_corr'].copy();z[~valid]=np.nan
                      ax.plot(a[arrkey+'_lag']/60,z,color=col,lw=1,label=method)
                  r=d['tests'][label+' / filtered'+suffix]
                  ax.axhline(r['null_correlation_q95'],color=R,ls='--',lw=1)
                  ax.set(xlabel='DG − CL reception lag (min)',ylabel='Pearson r',title=f"{label}: {title}\nmax r={r['correlation_r']:.3f}; IAAFT p={r['correlation_iaaft_p']:.3f}")
                  ax.grid(alpha=.13);ax.legend(fontsize=9,loc='lower right')
          finish(fig,'correlation-lag-searches','Time-shift correlation searches with whole-search controls',
                 'Red dashed: 95th percentile of the maximum over the whole IAAFT lag search; not a pointwise threshold.\nAt least 120 s overlap. Arrival-envelope screens are looser than the physically paired Monte Carlo test; lags do not uniquely determine location.')


      def catalogue_plot(events):
          data=[]
          for e in events[:19]:
              p=number(e.get('CL_full_record_max_p'))
              window=(c.utc(number(e['impact_earliest_s']))+'–'+c.utc(number(e['impact_latest_s']))) if e.get('impact_earliest_s') else 'No arc crossing'
              score=number(e['score_pm10s'])
              status='Edge guard' if e['guarded']=='True' else ('Burst in ±10 s' if p<.05 else 'Not above full-record control')
              data.append([e['time_utc'],e['bearing_deg'],f"{abs(number(e.get('arc_latitude'))):.2f} S" if e.get('arc_latitude') else '—',window,f'{score:.2f}' if np.isfinite(score) else '—',f'{p:.3f}' if np.isfinite(p) else '—',status])
          fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(16,10.5));ax.axis('off')
          table=ax.table(cellText=data,colLabels=['CL arrival\nUTC','Bearing\n°','Arc latitude','Conditional impact UTC\nexact reference arc','Max Z\n±10 s','CL global\nIAAFT p','Publication-trace result'],
                         cellLoc='left',colLoc='left',colWidths=[.095,.075,.095,.23,.08,.08,.275],bbox=[0,.02,1,.96])
          table.auto_set_font_size(False);table.set_fontsize(11)
          for (r,col),cell in table.get_celld().items():
              cell.set_edgecolor('#dedee4')
              if r==0:cell.set_facecolor(P);cell.set_text_props(color='white',weight='bold')
              else:cell.set_facecolor('#f5f4f8' if r%2 else 'white')
          finish(fig,'table1-event-audit','All nineteen Kadri Table 1 rows — reception, geometry and trace audit',
                 'Global p tests a plotted burst, not MH370 identity; windows near 00:51:50 and 00:54:30 can capture neighbouring major bursts.\nExact-arc impact envelopes are celerity endpoint ranges, not 95% intervals. Missing crossings are outside the chosen 20°S–45°S segment.')


      def event_zoom(events):
          traces=c.base.load_traces(c.BASECFG)
          targets=['00:39:02','00:39:23.700','00:49:58','00:53:31','00:52:04.350','00:54:30']
          fig,axes=plt.subplots(3,2,figsize=(14,11))
          for ax,target in zip(axes.ravel(),targets):
              e=next(e for e in events if e['time_utc']==target);tr=traces[e['panel']];t=tr.epoch_s-c.MIDNIGHT-number(e['arrival_s']);use=np.abs(t)<=20
              ax.plot(t[use],tr.pressure_pa[use],color=G,lw=1)
              ax.axvspan(-2,2,color=P,alpha=.09);ax.axvline(0,color=P,lw=1)
              ax.set(xlabel='Seconds relative to labelled reception time',ylabel='Published pressure (Pa)',title=f"CL {target} UTC\nmax Z ±10 s={number(e['score_pm10s']):.2f}; global p={number(e['CL_full_record_max_p']):.3f}")
              ax.grid(alpha=.15)
          finish(fig,'priority-event-close-ups','Priority candidates: pressure traces at the stated CL times',
                 'No new deconvolution or sharpening. The grey curves are interpolated publication vectors.\nFaint raw-array events may not survive plotting; lack of an isolated excursion here cannot contradict their presence in Kadri’s underlying data.')


      def write_report(d,events,energy):
          h=d['holland'];rows=[]
          for label,s in d['hypotheses'].items():
              rows.append(f"| {label} | {times(s['cl_arrival_s'])} | {times(s['dg_arrival_s'])} | {s['joint_coverage_fraction']:.1%} |")
          tests=[]
          for key,r in d['tests'].items():
              tests.append(f"| {key} | {r['correlation_r']:.3f} | {r['correlation_iaaft_p']:.3f} | {r['correlation_block_p']:.3f} | {r['joint_iaaft_p']:.3f} | {r['joint_block_p']:.3f} |")
          working=[r for r in energy if r['coupling_prior']=='working' and r['excess_loss_db']=='0']
          pressure='; '.join(f"{r['station']} {number(r['peak_q025_pa']):.2f}–{number(r['peak_q975_pa']):.2f} Pa peak (median {number(r['peak_median_pa']):.2f})" for r in working)
          chosen=[r for r in d['filter_rows'] if r['rank']==d['filter_rank']]
          suppression=' and '.join(f"{100*r['median_rms_removed_fraction']:.1f}%" for r in chosen)
          main=f'''# Conditional hydroacoustic tests — results and interpretation

      14 September 2026 · All event times UTC on 8 March 2014 · Exploratory publication-trace analysis

      ## What we can conclude

      We can find **timing-compatible excursions**, but these tests do not establish a common aircraft-impact signal at CL and DG. Results depend strongly on the background control. Broad timing searches initially select the well-known CL burst near 00:52:04, even though its published bearing is inconsistent with the tested southern-ocean sources. Excluding the two major bursts under their published-bearing interpretation is therefore a necessary sensitivity check. The surviving alignments still need raw-array bearing and nuisance-source checks; the selected coordinates are examples, not new source estimates.

      This is also **not a rejection of the unarrested-descent hypothesis**. The available drawings do not cover every predicted arrival, DG remains contaminated, and source coupling/path loss are uncalibrated. The pressure model overlaps the observed fluctuation scale rather than guaranteeing a detectable signal. No acoustic likelihood or posterior update has been created.

      [Browse all PNG graphics](graphics.html) · [Download the complete package](conditional-hydroacoustic-tests.zip) · [Detailed methods, assumptions and LaTeX](methods.html) · [Machine-readable results](results.json)

      ## 1. The agreed working coupling hypothesis

      We assume effective source coupling between **10⁻⁶ and 10⁻⁴ of normal kinetic energy**, with equal probability per logarithmic decade. This is 0.0001%–0.01%; its geometric midpoint is 0.001%. The F-35 comparison motivates the upper end as an optimistic analogue, not a universal aircraft efficiency. A 10⁻⁸–10⁻² control and four additional-loss settings show how sensitive the pressure result is. See the located source passages and equations in the methods.

      For the unarrested model, the central 95% normal-impact energy is **{h['normal_energy_j'][0]/1e9:.2f}–{h['normal_energy_j'][2]/1e9:.2f} GJ**. The corresponding effective coupled energy is **{h['coupled_energy_j'][0]/1000:.2f}–{h['coupled_energy_j'][2]/1000:.1f} kJ**, median **{h['coupled_energy_j'][1]/1000:.1f} kJ**. These are simulated prior-conditional ranges, not estimated MH370 energies.

      Under the explosion-equivalent pressure law with no additional loss: **{pressure}**. Ten additional dB reduces pressure by a factor of 3.16; twenty dB by ten. A separate ideal-duct energy-fluence/RMS calculation is included in the CSV, not pooled with that peak-pressure model. Neither is a calibrated receiver model.

      ![Timing, energy and pressure](holland-timing-energy-pressure.png)

      ## 2. Unarrested Holland descent: time and observable coverage

      With altitude uniform over 20,000–40,000 ft, a triangular distribution over Holland’s later final-transmission bounds, and no reduction in downward speed, impact falls within **{times(h['impact_time_s'])} UTC** for the central 95% of draws. That is **{h['impact_delay_s'][0]:.0f}–{h['impact_delay_s'][2]:.0f} seconds after 00:19:37.443**, median {h['impact_delay_s'][1]:.0f} seconds. The full support is {h['hard_delay_s'][0]:.0f}–{h['hard_delay_s'][1]:.0f} seconds. These intervals explicitly exclude phugoids and recovery. They do not apply to the longer-glide alternative.

      The starting location is a stand-alone, uniform-arclength control from 20°S to 45°S, not the current integrated flight PDF. Horizontal displacement is generated from the same fall time. Lower-altitude and uniform-rate sensitivities are in [the timing table](holland-timing-sensitivity.csv).

      | Source hypothesis | CL reception, central 95% | DG reception, central 95% | Both drawings cover the same draw |
      |---|---|---|---|
      {chr(10).join(rows)}

      For Pléiades, the intervals also marginalise a **uniform thirty-minute source-time control**. Coverage percentages refer to that declared prior and the guarded drawings, not probability that an event was or was not detected. In particular, missing late Pléiades receptions cannot be treated as acoustic silence.

      ![Arrival intervals and coverage](arrival-windows-and-coverage.png)

      ## 3. DG: best suppression under a signal-preservation constraint

      The selected leave-target-and-neighbours-out subspace has **rank {d['filter_rank']}**. It removes a median **{suppression}** of pulse-window RMS in the two DG panels while preserving the specified synthetic pulse shapes. This modest suppression is scientifically important: stronger filters remove substantially more of some injected signals. We therefore cannot describe the residual as airgun-free. “Best” means best among the tested subspaces subject to the declared preservation limits, not the best conceivable raw-data filter.

      ![Filtering trade-off and actual pressure](airgun-filter-trade-off.png)

      The former DG boundary feature around 01:10:00.5 is excluded by the panel-edge guard. We have not converted that plotting/filter-boundary artefact into a candidate.

      ## 4. Time-shift and joint-excursion results

      Each source family has 250,000 draws, separate CL/DG celerities of 1.43–1.57 km/s and a further ±5 s allowance at each station. We test raw DG and the selected filter, both before and after excluding the two major CL bursts under their published-bearing interpretation. We use all available CL panels, not just the visually attractive interval.

      The first statistic asks whether the **two score series correlate** somewhere in the admitted lag range. The second asks whether **both predicted reception times have an unusually large score**. These are distinct questions. The second is easily dominated by one real CL burst finding one of many DG airgun excursions.

      IAAFT controls preserve the CL pressure distribution and approximately its spectrum while scrambling phases. Block controls preserve actual short-time bursts and move them within their original ten-minute panels. Thus an IAAFT-only result can indicate a real transient without demonstrating unusual cross-station association. We do not select whichever null gives a smaller p-value.

      | Search | Maximum r | Correlation p, IAAFT | Correlation p, blocks | Joint-score p, IAAFT | Joint-score p, blocks |
      |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
      {chr(10).join(tests)}

      These displayed p-values already search the full allowed lag/source domain, but are **before** the additional correction across 32 current search choices. Both corrected IAAFT columns are supplied in [hypothesis-tests.csv](hypothesis-tests.csv). Neither null supplies a probability that a candidate is MH370. The 399 controls give p-value resolution 0.0025; zero exceedances do not mean zero false-alarm probability. The repeated historical selection of these drawings remains an additional limitation.

      ![Lag scans and whole-search controls](correlation-lag-searches.png)

      The aligned plots show central travel-time examples at the BRAN, OSCAR and GLORYS modes. The strongest surviving-pair panels separately show the best *sampled timing-compatible* examples after the major-burst veto. The latter must not be presented as confirmed detections, preferred locations or agreement with any unverified faint-event bearing.

      ## 5. Catalogue candidates and priorities for Kadri

      All nineteen Table 1 rows are tested, plus four clearly labelled project re-picks. The main priorities remain:

      1. **00:49:58 / 260.41° and 00:53:31 / 257.58°:** the plotted traces do not show a strong isolated excursion at these catalogue times under the current full-record control. Ask Kadri to identify the precise raw-array intervals and reproduce the bearings. Their conditional timing/geometry interest does not validate their waveform identity.
      2. **00:54:30 / 306.18°:** a plotted burst is present near the preferred event, but its own reported bearing points to a substantially more northerly reference-arc intersection than the Pléiades surfaces. Use its own implied impact/DG window, not a southern-source pairing selected only for visual timing agreement. The project pick near 00:54:26.95 is not an independent event.
      3. **00:39:02 / 268.24° and nearby early CL features:** the catalogue event and the project pick at 00:39:23.7 are different timestamps and cannot be merged without raw data. The exact-arc/celerity timing is explicitly tabulated; it is not automatically the unarrested window.
      4. **00:52:04-region / reported 57°:** retain as a useful nuisance/direction control, not a southern-source candidate, unless the assignment of bearing to waveform is revised using the raw triad.
      5. **Remaining Table 1 rows and the surviving paired excursions:** retain the full register to avoid cherry-picking. A large DG residual within a several-minute window is not a second-station verification. Candidate bearings need raw-array uncertainty and event-identity checks.

      The earlier slide’s “CL 00:38–00:55” is the approximate catalogue span, **not two additional detected events**. The full current CSV records every source label, exact timestamp convention, ±2/5/10 s score, guarded availability, conditional arc intersection, implied impact envelope and largest DG excursion in its predicted window.

      ![Catalogue event audit](table1-event-audit.png)

      ![Priority event close-ups](priority-event-close-ups.png)

      ![Reported bearings and Pléiades conditionals](catalogue-bearings-and-pleiades.png)

      ## 6. How this should enter the paper and the collaboration

      The useful result is a reproducible **conditional hypothesis test with declared limits**: there are real plotted transients, but the present cross-station timing evidence is confounded by repeated DG pulses, event selection and uncertain propagation. Conditional energy predictions overlap the plotted noise/transient scale and cannot turn missing PDF peaks into a rejection of high-energy impact.

      The next discriminating work is not another maximum over the same drawings. It is a raw-triad rerun of this fixed event register, bearing uncertainty, DG spatial/periodic suppression, and source-state injection–recovery through the actual processing chain. Only then can a source-dependent detection/non-detection model contribute acoustic weights to the Bayesian estimator. The present estimator is unchanged.

      ## Reproduction and checks

      Run from the repository root:

      ```bash
      OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 .venv/bin/python -B .sources/kadri-2024-hydroacoustics/code/conditional_hypothesis_tests.py
      .venv/bin/python -B .sources/kadri-2024-hydroacoustics/code/conditional_hypothesis_report.py
      ```

      Seven focused numerical/boundary checks passed. The analysis took {d['wall_seconds']:.1f} s, with maximum resident memory {d['maximum_rss_mib']:.0f} MiB in this environment. Seed: {d['configuration']['seed']}. Median IAAFT relative amplitude-spectrum error: {d['null_controls']['iaaft_spectrum_relative_error'][1]:.4g}. The full input/code hash manifest is in [results.json](results.json). Detailed assumptions, mathematical definitions and located sources are in [methods.html](methods.html). See [graphics-checks.json](graphics-checks.json) for automated canvas checks; all main graphics are also inspected visually.
      '''
          (OUT/'report.md').write_text(main)
          methods=(c.ROOT/'conditional-tests-methods.md').read_text()
