      +## Executed conditional tests with a working coupling hypothesis
      +
      +The reproducible stand-alone tests cover the separate BRAN, OSCAR and
      +GLORYS/WAVERYS Pléiades transport conditionals, an explicitly unarrested Holland
      +descent, all nineteen Table 1 rows and labelled project re-picks. They compare
      +unfiltered DG with a preservation-constrained held-out pulse-subspace filter,
      +source-time Monte Carlo pairing and lag correlation, phase-randomised and
      +burst-preserving controls, and imported-bearing sensitivities. Nothing is
      +promoted to an estimator likelihood. Coupling and pressure ranges remain
      +declared working hypotheses; see `conditional-tests-methods.md`.

      +### Comparing that pressure scale with the actual excursions
      +
      +The following diagnostic evaluates the same marginal coupled-energy prior at the locations of the selected Holland timing examples, then overlays the maximum absolute plotted pressure within ±4 s of their predicted arrivals. The diamonds include all background and interference, not an isolated aircraft contribution. In particular, a large DG pulse can exceed the working source range without making a smaller superimposed impact signal impossible. Conversely, amplitude overlap at CL does not identify its origin.
      +
      +The event searches deliberately **do not require** the total recorded pressure to equal a predicted source-only pressure, because doing so would treat airgun/noise energy as aircraft energy. This comparison is a pressure-scale check, not an amplitude likelihood, detectability calibration or energy estimate for an individual event.

    from pathlib import Path
    import numpy as np
    for p in sorted(Path('.sources/iannello-2016-end-of-flight/data/cases').glob('*.csv')):
     a=np.genfromtxt(p,delimiter=',',skip_header=1);t=a[:,0];h=a[:,3];w=-np.gradient(h,t)*60
     good=(w>=13800)&(w<=25300); earlier=np.r_[np.full(8,np.nan),w[:-8]];pair=good&(earlier>=2900)&(earlier<=14800)&(w>earlier)
     pair[:9]=False;pair[-1]=False
     ix=np.flatnonzero(pair)
     if not len(ix):continue
     print(p.stem)
     for grp in np.split(ix,np.flatnonzero(np.diff(ix)>1)+1):
      first,last=grp[0],grp[-1]
      futureclimb=max(np.maximum.accumulate(h[first:][::-1])[::-1]-h[first:])
      print('epoch',first,last,'height',h[first],h[last], 'delay to last',t[-1]-last,t[-1]-first,'earlierW',earlier[first],earlier[last],'laterW',w[first],w[last], 'maxlaterclimb',futureclimb, 'minremainingW',min(w[first:]),'highalt',np.sum((h[grp]>=20000)&(h[grp]<=40000)))

  ── Assistant
  There’s an important result in the trajectory files: a high descent rate can be followed by a substantial recovery before the final dive. Conversely, if we align 00:19 with the final steep segment, the aircraft can already be much lower than the 20,000–40,000 ft assumed in our previous calculation.

  I’m separating those two alignments in the new chart. They give illustrative timing envelopes, not Boeing-derived 95% probabilities; the public files lack case weights and complete configuration details.

    ── Created .sources/kadri-2024-hydroacoustics/code/boeing_descent_crosscheck.py +154
      #!/usr/bin/env python3
      """Source-side timing check of public Boeing exports, not a flight likelihood.

      Re-time rate-bound-compatible samples; do not identify CSV zero with flameout.
      No probability weights or true phugoid labels are available for these ten cases.
      """
      from __future__ import annotations
      import csv
      import hashlib
      import json
      from pathlib import Path
      import resource
      import time

      import numpy as np

      ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
      CASE_ROOT=ROOT.parent/'iannello-2016-end-of-flight'
      OUT=ROOT/'outputs'/'conditional-hypothesis-tests'
      REFERENCE_S=19*60+37.443
      ARCHIVE_SHA='e400ac73478dff8698cd344a69d7969804f6adb0eb2121d5cacd3b58d63111db'
      STATIONS={'CL':(-34.892,114.141),'DG':(-7.639,72.484)}


      def digest(path):
          return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


      def rate(h,width=2):
          """Centred slope of integer-foot samples, downward ft/min; width in seconds."""
          half=width//2
          w=np.full(len(h),np.nan)
          w[half:-half]=-60*(h[width:]-h[:-width])/width
          return w


      def later_gain(h):
          """Largest subsequent altitude gain anywhere after each indexed sample."""
          gain=np.maximum.accumulate(h[::-1])[::-1]-h
          return np.maximum.accumulate(gain[::-1])[::-1]


      def candidates(a,width=2,min_accel_g=None):
          h=a[:,3];w=rate(h,width)
          prev=np.r_[np.full(8,np.nan),w[:-8]]
          acceleration=(w-prev)/(8*60*32.174)
          keep=(prev>=2900)&(prev<=14800)&(w>=13800)&(w<=25300)&(w>prev)
          if min_accel_g is not None:
              keep &= acceleration>=min_accel_g
          return np.flatnonzero(keep),w,prev,acceleration


      def envelope(rows,key):
          return [float(min(r[key] for r in rows)),float(max(r[key] for r in rows))]


      def range_km(lat,lon,station):
          p1,p2=np.radians([lat,station[0]])
          dl=np.radians(station[1]-lon)
          return float(2*6371.0088*np.arcsin(np.sqrt(np.sin((p2-p1)/2)**2+np.cos(p1)*np.cos(p2)*np.sin(dl/2)**2)))


      def write_csv(path,rows):
          with path.open('w',newline='') as f:
              writer=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]));writer.writeheader();writer.writerows(rows)


      def main():
          start=time.perf_counter()
          archive=CASE_ROOT/'data'/'boeing-eof-simulations-2018-08-19.zip'
          assert digest(archive)==ARCHIVE_SHA
          files=sorted((CASE_ROOT/'data'/'cases').glob('Case ??.csv'))
          assert len(files)==10
          arrays={};rows=[];case_rows=[];sens=[]
          for path in files:
              case=int(path.stem.split()[-1]);a=np.genfromtxt(path,delimiter=',',skip_header=1)
              assert a.shape[1]==4 and np.all(np.diff(a[:,0])==1) and a[0,0]==0
              arrays[case]=a;h=a[:,3];ix,w,prev,accel=candidates(a)
              gain=later_gain(h)
              # Completion to zero altitude is only an explicit final-slope control.
              slopes=np.array([(h[-1-k]-h[-1])/k for k in [1,5,10]])
              tails=h[-1]/slopes if len(ix) and np.all(slopes>0) else None
              case_rows.append({'case':case,'sample_count':len(a),'file_duration_s':a[-1,0],
                                'initial_altitude_ft':h[0],'last_altitude_ft':h[-1],
                                'peak_downward_rate_fpm':float(np.nanmax(w)),
                                'rate_bound_match_count':len(ix),
                                'completion_min_s':None if tails is None else float(min(tails)),
                                'completion_max_s':None if tails is None else float(max(tails)),
                                'input_sha256':digest(path)})
              for i in ix:
                  assert tails is not None
                  # An altitude-only label, not a certification of a phugoid mode.
                  branch='recovery_after_match' if gain[i]>=100 else 'final_descending_leg'
                  delay=a[-1,0]-a[i,0]
                  rows.append({'case':case,'branch':branch,'alignment_file_s':a[i,0],
                               'alignment_altitude_ft':h[i],'earlier_rate_fpm':prev[i],
                               'later_rate_fpm':w[i],'acceleration_8s_g':accel[i],
                               'largest_subsequent_gain_ft':gain[i],
                               'delay_to_last_record_s':delay,
                               'completion_min_s':float(min(tails)),
                               'completion_max_s':float(max(tails)),
                               'projected_contact_delay_min_s':delay+min(tails),
                               'projected_contact_delay_max_s':delay+max(tails)})
              for width in [2,4,8]:
                  for amin in [None,.5,.67]:
                      jj,_,_,_=candidates(a,width,amin)
                      if len(jj):
                          sens.append({'case':case,'derivative_width_s':width,
                                       'minimum_acceleration_g':amin,
                                       'matching_samples':len(jj),
                                       'min_delay_to_last_s':a[-1,0]-a[jj[-1],0],
                                       'max_delay_to_last_s':a[-1,0]-a[jj[0],0]})
          assert sorted({r['case'] for r in rows})==[3,4,5,6,10]
          branches={}
          for branch in ['final_descending_leg','recovery_after_match']:
              group=[r for r in rows if r['branch']==branch]
              branches[branch]={
                  'case_count':len({r['case'] for r in group}),'sample_count':len(group),
                  'delay_to_last_record_s':envelope(group,'delay_to_last_record_s'),
                  'projected_contact_delay_s':[min(r['projected_contact_delay_min_s'] for r in group),max(r['projected_contact_delay_max_s'] for r in group)],
                  'alignment_altitude_ft':envelope(group,'alignment_altitude_ft'),
                  'cases':[{'case':case,'delay_to_last_record_s':envelope([r for r in group if r['case']==case],'delay_to_last_record_s'),
                            'alignment_altitude_ft':envelope([r for r in group if r['case']==case],'alignment_altitude_ft')}
                           for case in sorted({r['case'] for r in group})]}
          prior=json.loads((OUT/'results.json').read_text())
          spatial=ROOT.parent/'pleiades-bran2016-forward-inversion'/'outputs'/'conditional-impact-density.csv'
          with spatial.open() as f:
              spatial_rows=list(csv.DictReader(f))
          # Explicit location example inherited from the previously shown OSCAR panel;
          # not a geographic placement recovered from the Boeing coordinates.
          location={'label':'OSCAR conditional transport mode (fixed illustration)',
                    'latitude':-34.916713,'longitude':92.047164}
          receptions=[]
          for label,window in [(b,v['projected_contact_delay_s']) for b,v in branches.items()]+[
                  ('constant_rate_prior_95',prior['holland']['impact_delay_s'][::2]),
                  ('ATSB_approximately_18_minutes_after_reference',[18*60,18*60])]:
              for station,position in STATIONS.items():
                  r=range_km(location['latitude'],location['longitude'],position)
                  receptions.append({'model':label,'station':station,'range_km':r,
                                     'earliest_arrival_s':REFERENCE_S+window[0]+r/1.57-5,
                                     'latest_arrival_s':REFERENCE_S+window[1]+r/1.43+5})
          # Small independent controls: exact linear/quadratic slopes and recovery labels.
          x=np.arange(30.)
          assert np.allclose(rate(30000-250*x)[1:-1],15000)
          assert np.allclose(rate(30000-x*x)[1:-1],120*x[1:-1])
          assert later_gain(np.array([300.,200.,100.]))[0]==0
          assert later_gain(np.array([300.,100.,250.,0.]))[0]==150
          for row in rows:
              assert row['projected_contact_delay_min_s']>row['delay_to_last_record_s']
          assert range_km(-34.892,114.141,STATIONS['CL'])==0
          data={
              'status':'RATE-BOUND TIMING CONTROL; NOT JOINT BFO FIT, CASE PROBABILITIES OR HYDROACOUSTIC LIKELIHOOD',
              'reference_utc':'2014-03-08T00:19:37.443Z','reference_seconds_utc':REFERENCE_S,
              'configuration':{'rate_derivative_width_s':2,'earlier_sample_offset_s':8,
                               'earlier_rate_bounds_fpm':[2900,14800],'later_rate_bounds_fpm':[13800,25300],
                               'subsequent_recovery_threshold_ft':100,'completion_slope_windows_s':[1,5,10],
                               'source_reference_epoch_note':'Eight-second separation rounded as in Holland; not a full BFO re-evaluation.',
                               'celerity_km_s':[1.43,1.57],'station_extra_timing_s':5},
              'cases':case_rows,'branches':branches,'prior_comparator':prior['holland'],
              'reception_example_location':location,'receptions':receptions,
              'focused_checks':['archive identity and ten sequential case files','independent linear slope',
                                'independent quadratic slope','monotonic/recovery classification',
                                'positive completion follows last sample','zero-distance geometry',
                                'five-case partition independently reproduced'],
              'input_sha256':{str(p):digest(p) for p in [*files,archive,OUT/'results.json',Path(__file__)]},
              'numpy_version':np.__version__,'random_seed':None,'stochastic_sampling':'none',
              'runtime_s':time.perf_counter()-start,'maximum_rss_mib':resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss/1024}
          (OUT/'boeing-descent-crosscheck.json').write_text(json.dumps(data,indent=2)+'\n')
          write_csv(OUT/'boeing-rate-bound-alignments.csv',rows)
          write_csv(OUT/'boeing-timing-sensitivity.csv',sens)
          write_csv(OUT/'boeing-reception-windows.csv',receptions)
          print(json.dumps({'branches':branches,'receptions':receptions,'checks':len(data['focused_checks'])},indent=2))


      if __name__=='__main__':main()

      +def boeing_plots(b):
      +    branches=b['branches']
      +    order=['final_descending_leg','recovery_after_match','constant_rate_prior_95']
      +    labels=['Final descending leg\n(no subsequent climb)',
      +            'Earlier high-rate episode\n(subsequent recovery)',
      +            'Previous constant-rate model\n(20,000–40,000 ft)']
      +    fig,axes=plt.subplots(2,1,figsize=(13,10),gridspec_kw={'height_ratios':[1,1.35]})
      +    ax=axes[0]
      +    for i,(key,col) in enumerate(zip(order,[P,R,G])):
      +        if key in branches:
      +            row=branches[key];lo,hi=row['projected_contact_delay_s']
      +            ax.plot(row['delay_to_last_record_s'],[i]*2,color=col,lw=7)
      +            ax.plot([lo,hi],[i]*2,color=col,lw=2)
      +            ax.plot([lo,hi],[i]*2,'o',color='white',mec=col,ms=6)
      +            text=f'{lo:.0f}–{hi:.0f} s (case envelope)'
      +        else:
      +            lo,hi=np.array(b['prior_comparator']['impact_delay_s'])[[0,2]]
      +            ax.plot([lo,hi],[i]*2,color=col,lw=7)
      +            text=f'{lo:.0f}–{hi:.0f} s (prior-conditional 95%)'
      +        ax.text(180,i,text,ha='left',va='center',fontsize=10)
      +    ax.set(yticks=range(3),yticklabels=labels,xlim=(0,284),ylim=(2.6,-.6),
      +           xticks=range(0,181,30),xlabel='Seconds after the chosen 00:19:37.443 alignment',
      +           title='A. Rate-bound re-timing: sample envelopes are not probability intervals')
      +    ax.grid(axis='x',alpha=.15)
      +    ax=axes[1]
      +    order+=['ATSB_approximately_18_minutes_after_reference']
      +    labels+=['ATSB longer-flight illustration\n(about +18 min; not a high-rate fit)']
      +    for i,key in enumerate(order):
      +        for station,offset,col in [('CL',-.13,P),('DG',.13,R)]:
      +            r=next(r for r in b['receptions'] if r['model']==key and r['station']==station)
      +            ax.plot(np.array([r['earliest_arrival_s'],r['latest_arrival_s']])/60,[i+offset]*2,
      +                    color=col,lw=6,label=station if i==0 else None)
      +    ax.axvline(57,color=P,ls=':',lw=1.2)
      +    ax.axvline(60,color=R,ls=':',lw=1.2)
      +    ax.text(56.6,-.65,'CL drawings end',ha='right',color=P,fontsize=9)
      +    ax.text(60.4,-.65,'DG drawings start',ha='left',color=R,fontsize=9)
      +    ax.set(yticks=range(4),yticklabels=labels,xlim=(38,82),ylim=(3.6,-1.2),
      +           xlabel='UTC reception time, 8 March 2014',
      +           title='B. Fixed OSCAR location only: 34.9167°S, 92.0472°E; CL 2,011 km / DG 3,632 km')
      +    ax.set_xticks([40,45,50,55,60,65,70,75,80]);clock_axis(ax)
      +    ax.legend(loc='upper right',ncol=2,frameon=False,fontsize=10)
      +    ax.grid(axis='x',alpha=.15)
      +    finish(fig,'boeing-impact-and-reception-windows','Boeing cross-check: what timing windows are supported?',
      +           'A: thick coloured bars end at the last exported sample; open endpoints add an explicit 1/5/10-second slope completion to sea level.\nB: c = 1.43–1.57 km/s and ±5 s per station; fixed-location envelopes, not marginal 95% receptions. About +18 min = 20 min minus 2 min.\nRe-timing is not a joint BFO/system-clock fit. A recovery label is not proof of a phugoid; no new acoustic search is performed here.')
      +
      +    # Display actual exported altitude histories: no generative redraw of data.
      +    source=c.ROOT.parent/'iannello-2016-end-of-flight'/'data'/'cases'
      +    rows=readcsv('boeing-rate-bound-alignments.csv')
      +    fig,axes=plt.subplots(2,1,figsize=(13,9))
      +    for ax,case in zip(axes,[3,6]):
      +        a=np.genfromtxt(source/f'Case {case:02d}.csv',delimiter=',',skip_header=1)
      +        selected=[r for r in rows if int(r['case'])==case]
      +        t0=min(float(r['alignment_file_s']) for r in selected)
      +        ax.plot(a[:,0]-t0,a[:,3]/1000,color='#343c47',lw=2,label='Boeing exported altitude')
      +        for branch,col,label in [('recovery_after_match',R,'Earlier allowable alignment'),
      +                                 ('final_descending_leg',P,'Final-leg allowable alignment')]:
      +            g=[r for r in selected if r['branch']==branch]
      +            start=min(float(r['alignment_file_s']) for r in g)-t0
      +            stop=max(float(r['alignment_file_s']) for r in g)-t0
      +            ax.axvspan(start,stop,color=col,alpha=.18,label=label)
      +            ax.axvline(start,color=col,lw=1)
      +        ax.plot(a[-1,0]-t0,a[-1,3]/1000,'o',mfc='white',mec='#343c47',ms=7)
      +        first=next(r for r in selected if float(r['alignment_file_s'])==t0)
      +        gain=float(first['largest_subsequent_gain_ft'])
      +        ax.text(.98,.91,f"Later altitude recovery: {gain:,.0f} ft\nLast record: {a[-1,3]:,.0f} ft, not water contact",transform=ax.transAxes,
      +                va='top',ha='right',fontsize=11,bbox={'facecolor':'white','edgecolor':'#ddd','alpha':.95,'pad':6})
      +        ax.set(xlim=(-15,a[-1,0]-t0+10),ylim=(0,40 if case==3 else 36),ylabel='Altitude (thousands of ft)',
      +               xlabel='Seconds relative to the first rate-bound-compatible sample (not the simulator start)',
      +               title=f'Case {case:02d}: same trajectory, different candidate placements of the final BFO epoch')
      +        ax.grid(alpha=.15)
      +    fig.legend(*axes[0].get_legend_handles_labels(),loc='upper center',bbox_to_anchor=(.52,.94),ncol=3,fontsize=10,frameon=False)
      +    finish(fig,'boeing-recovery-altitude-histories','High descent rate does not necessarily mean no subsequent recovery',
      +           'Shaded samples meet only the two published rate bounds eight seconds apart, with increasing downward speed. No joint BFO likelihood is fitted.\nATSB warns that some trajectories leave the simulator database. Altitude alone cannot distinguish a true phugoid from bank/pitch coupling.\nThese are existing Boeing exports, not new Boeing runs with a phugoid switch turned on or off.')
      +

      +def boeing_summary(b):
      +    def utcspan(values):
      +        return '–'.join(c.utc(round(x)) for x in values)
      +    branches=b['branches'];ref=b['reference_seconds_utc'];summary=[]
      +    for key,label in [('final_descending_leg','Final descending leg; no subsequent climb'),
      +                      ('recovery_after_match','Earlier high-rate episode followed by recovery')]:
      +        row=branches[key];low,high=row['projected_contact_delay_s']
      +        summary.append(f"| {label} | {row['delay_to_last_record_s'][0]:.0f}–{row['delay_to_last_record_s'][1]:.0f} s | approximately {low:.0f}–{high:.0f} s | {utcspan([ref+low,ref+high])} |")
      +    case_table=[]
      +    for case in [3,4,5,6,10]:
      +        a=next(r for r in branches['final_descending_leg']['cases'] if r['case']==case)
      +        r=next(r for r in branches['recovery_after_match']['cases'] if r['case']==case)
      +        case_table.append(f"| {case:02d} | {a['delay_to_last_record_s'][0]:.0f}–{a['delay_to_last_record_s'][1]:.0f} | {a['alignment_altitude_ft'][0]:,.0f}–{a['alignment_altitude_ft'][1]:,.0f} | {r['delay_to_last_record_s'][0]:.0f}–{r['delay_to_last_record_s'][1]:.0f} |")
      +    reception=[]
      +    for key,label in [('final_descending_leg','Final descending leg'),('recovery_after_match','Recovery after initial high-rate episode'),
      +                      ('ATSB_approximately_18_minutes_after_reference','Illustration at about +18 min')]:
      +        pair=[next(r for r in b['receptions'] if r['model']==key and r['station']==s) for s in ['CL','DG']]
      +        reception.append(f"| {label} | {utcspan([pair[0]['earliest_arrival_s'],pair[0]['latest_arrival_s']])} | {utcspan([pair[1]['earliest_arrival_s'],pair[1]['latest_arrival_s']])} |")
      +    return f'''# Boeing cross-check: descent, recovery and impact timing
      +
      +14 September 2026 · UTC on 8 March 2014 · [Main report](report.html) · [All graphics](graphics.html)
      +
      +## Bottom line
      +
      +**Our previous 58–139-second interval is not a general 95% bound for an unarrested descent.** It is the central 95% of an assumed 20,000–40,000 ft, constant-rate model. Boeing's public trajectories include lower-altitude final descents, and episodes where a high descent rate is followed by recovery. Those are useful checks on the assumed height and subsequent dynamics.
      +
      +I re-analysed the ten one-second coordinate/altitude files released by Victor Iannello with ATSB permission, rather than estimating timings from plot pixels. [Dataset provenance and original release](https://mh370.radiantphysics.com/2018/08/19/end-of-flight-simulations-of-mh370/). The CSV clock is **not automatically the flameout clock**; engine, electrical and control-state timestamps are not supplied. This analysis instead asks where a pair of rate-bound-compatible samples could be aligned to 00:19:29 and 00:19:37. It does not demonstrate that a case satisfies the complete SATCOM and systems sequence.
      +
      +## Short-window results: what “with/without phugoids” can mean here
      +
      +The columns below distinguish the recorded time remaining from a **modelled completion** to sea level. The selected files stop 484–1,131 ft above their altitude datum, not at water contact. Completion holds the final slope from each of the last 1, 5 and 10 seconds constant; the envelope of those alternatives is included below. It adds roughly 1–2 seconds, but does not validate behaviour beyond the simulator database.
      +
      +| Subsequent behaviour | Time to last exported point | Including final-slope completion | Illustrative impact UTC |
      +|---|---|---|---|
      +{chr(10).join(summary)}
      +
      +These are **outer spans of discrete case/sample timings, not continuous support, 95% intervals, or exhaustive physical limits**. Each category contains the same five cases (03, 04, 05, 06, 10), aligned to different parts of their histories. “Without” here means no subsequent climb after the chosen alignment; it does not mean the entire flight had no phugoids or that descent speed remains constant. “With” allows subsequent altitude recovery. Position/altitude exports cannot uniquely label a phugoid, and I have not run paired simulations with phugoids artificially enabled/disabled.
      +
      +![Impact and station reception windows](boeing-impact-and-reception-windows.png)
      +
      +| Case | Final-leg seconds to last point | Height at final-leg alignment (ft) | Earlier/recovery seconds to last point |
      +|---|---|---|---|
      +{chr(10).join(case_table)}
      +
      +Most final-leg matches lie **below our previous 20,000 ft lower limit**. This is why their remaining time can be much shorter. A high descent rate at 00:19 does not itself determine altitude, and fixing the rate to impact misses subsequent acceleration as well as recovery. The old coupled-energy/pressure prior therefore also remains a separate working model; it is not recalibrated by this timing check.
      +
      +![Actual exported altitude histories](boeing-recovery-altitude-histories.png)
      +
      +## What about the longer, phugoid-inclusive flights?
      +
      +[ATSB's 2016 report, corrected August 2017, printed pp. 7–8](https://www.atsb.gov.au/sites/default/files/media/5773389/ae-2014-054_mh370-search-and-debris-update_aug2017.pdf) reports phugoids in some runs and airborne durations of about 20 minutes after the second engine flameout. Under its nominal two-minute flameout-to-final-transmission convention, that corresponds to **about 18 minutes after the final transmission: roughly 00:37:30 UTC**. This is useful longer-flight context, **not** a 95th/99th percentile, a maximum glide limit, or proof that these longer flights match Holland's final high/increasing rates at the nominal log-on time. The report cautions that its scenarios are non-exhaustive and that some trajectories exceed the simulation database.
      +
      +It would therefore be wrong either to rule out all longer flight from the short timings above, or to claim that Boeing established another 18–20 minutes of flight *after an observed high-rate terminal segment*. For now, retain a short terminal-descent family and a separate recovery/longer-flight family, with explicit alignment assumptions and without arbitrary probability weights.
      +
      +## What would arrive at CL and DG?
      +
      +For a fixed illustrative source at the **OSCAR Pléiades-conditional mode, 34.9167°S, 92.0472°E**, the windows are:
      +
      +| Timing family | Cape Leeuwin (CL) | Diego Garcia South (DG) |
      +|---|---|---|
      +{chr(10).join(reception)}
      +
      +These use 2,011 km to CL and 3,632 km to DG, effective celerity 1.43–1.57 km/s, and ±5 seconds per station. They are endpoint envelopes **at this location only**, not 95% station windows over the full source PDF or a bathymetric/AGW calculation. The +18-minute row is an illustrative late impact, not the whole longer-flight reception interval. Some early DG arrivals precede the 01:00 start of the recovered drawings; some late CL arrivals follow their 00:57 end. Those gaps are unavailable data, not acoustic silence.
      +
      +**Scope:** the previous acoustic pairing, significance tests and energy simulations have not been rerun against these enlarged timing alternatives. Their reported results still refer to their original declared search. This check supplies a more appropriate set of timing controls for a subsequent expanded search; it produces neither a new signal detection nor a posterior update.
      +
      +## Method and mathematical definition
      +
      +Downward speed is the two-second centred difference of one-second, integer-foot altitude samples:
      +
      +\\[
      +w_i=-60\\frac{{h_{{i+1}}-h_{{i-1}}}}{{2}}\\quad[\\mathrm{{ft\\,min^{{-1}}}}].
      +\\]
      +
      +We screen for \\(2,900\\le w_{{i-8}}\\le14,800\\), \\(13,800\\le w_i\\le25,300\\) ft/min, and \\(w_i>w_{{i-8}}\\). These are [Holland's Table VII bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/1702.02432v3), **not** a joint BFO likelihood: the rectangle ignores shared oscillator/track errors. A later gain of at least 100 ft distinguishes the recovery category. Every final-leg selected segment is monotonically descending in the exported record. With last-sample time \\(T\\), height \\(h_T\\), and final downward slope \\(v_{{T,k}}\\) in ft/s:
      +
      +\\[
      +\\Delta t_{{\\rm record}}=T-t_i,\\qquad
      +\\Delta t_{{\\rm contact},k}}=T-t_i+\\frac{{h_T}}{{v_{{T,k}}}},\\quad
      +v_{{T,k}}=\\frac{{h_{{T-k}}-h_T}}{{k}},\\quad k\\in\\{{1,5,10\\}}\\;\\mathrm{{s}}.
      +\\]
      +
      +Four/eight-second rate derivatives and minimum eight-second downward accelerations of 0.5 g and 0.67 g are **separate sensitivity screens**, not probabilities. Without an acceleration floor the combined time-to-last-point envelope stays 9–159 s for all three derivative widths. At 0.67 g it becomes 12–127 s for the two/four-second derivatives and 13–127 s for eight seconds. Thus the longer end of the loose rate-bound envelope is sensitive to enforcing an acceleration condition; 0.67 g is not asserted to be a calibrated acceptance threshold. Full results: [sample alignments](boeing-rate-bound-alignments.csv), [sensitivity table](boeing-timing-sensitivity.csv), [station windows](boeing-reception-windows.csv), [provenance and checks](boeing-descent-crosscheck.json).
      +
      +The ten engineered cases have no sampling probabilities; a percentile over their rows would largely reflect time spent in each selected state. Seven focused numerical/provenance checks pass. The main methods document contains the located source ledger. Reproduce with `boeing_descent_crosscheck.py`, then `conditional_hypothesis_report.py` as documented in the source README.
      +'''
      +

      +## Boeing trajectory timing cross-check (separate from acoustic tests)
      +
      +The cross-check reads the ten one-second altitude/coordinate exports made public
      +by Iannello in 2018 with ATSB permission. It does not use the CSV time origin as a
      +flameout timestamp. A two-second centred derivative supplies downward rate, and
      +candidate pairs eight seconds apart are screened against Holland's Table VII
      +intervals, with increasing downward speed. The rectangular screen does not retain
      +shared oscillator, heading or error correlations; it is not a joint BFO fit.
      +It does not match the nominal electrical/log-on clock. Rates differentiated over
      +four/eight seconds and minimum accelerations of 0.5/0.67 g are separate controls.
      +
      +A subsequent altitude recovery of at least 100 ft labels an earlier/recovery
      +episode. Every retained final-leg segment is monotonically descending thereafter;
      +this does not mean constant downward speed or no earlier oscillations. Neither
      +classification establishes a true aerodynamic phugoid from altitude alone.
      +The selected files terminate above the ocean; completion uses the final downward
      +slope measured over 1, 5 or 10 seconds as explicit alternatives:
      +
      +\[
      +w_i=-60\frac{h_{i+1}-h_{i-1}}{2}\quad[\mathrm{ft\,min^{-1}}],\qquad
      +\Delta t_{\mathrm{contact},k}=T-t_i+\frac{h_T}{(h_{T-k}-h_T)/k},
      +\quad k\in\{1,5,10\}\;\mathrm{s}.
      +\]
      +
      +Reported spans are minima/maxima over discrete selected samples, not continuous
      +support or probability intervals. None of the ten runs has an empirical weight.
      +The file hashes, assumptions, sensitivity outputs and checks are recorded in
      +`boeing-descent-crosscheck.json`; the original hydroacoustic `results.json` is
      +unchanged. The source/pressure priors and chance-coincidence tests must not be
      +described as having been rerun or validated by this timing comparison.
      +
      +ATSB's approximate 20-minute airborne examples are relative to second-engine
      +flameout. Subtracting the nominal two-minute log-on delay yields approximately
      +18 minutes after the final transmission under that systems hypothesis, not a
      +new 95th/99th percentile or proof of that much flight after a high-rate BFO epoch.
      +
      +### Located Boeing/ATSB source passages
      +
      +| Claim | Retrieved source | Locator | Short quote / exact data | Status |
      +|---|---|---|---|---|
      +| Nominal reference delay | [ATSB, 2 November 2016, corrected 18 August 2017](https://www.atsb.gov.au/sites/default/files/media/5773389/ae-2014-054_mh370-search-and-debris-update_aug2017.pdf) | Printed p. 7 / PDF p. 11, paragraph above Fig. 6 | “two minutes after” | FOUND in retrieved full PDF |
      +| Longer simulated durations | Same ATSB report | Printed p. 7, third result bullet | “approximately 20 minutes” | FOUND |
      +| Oscillations occur in some cases | Same ATSB report | Printed p. 8 / PDF p. 12, second bullet and footnote 2 | “phugoid motion” | FOUND |
      +| Simulator validity caution | Same ATSB report | Printed p. 8, third bullet | “outside the simulation database” | FOUND |

    from pathlib import Path
    import base64,hashlib,io,json,re,zipfile
    from PIL import Image
    out=Path('.sources/kadri-2024-hydroacoustics/outputs/conditional-hypothesis-tests')
    for p in sorted(out.glob('*.html')):
     t=p.read_text();assert p.stat().st_size<4_500_000
     imgs=re.findall(r'src="data:image/(jpeg|png);base64,(["'^"]+)"'"',t)
     for typ,v in imgs:
      im=Image.open(io.BytesIO(base64.b64decode(v)));im.verify()
     for v in re.findall(r'(?:href|src)="(["'^"]+)"'"',t):
      if ':' not in v and not v.startswith('#'):
       assert (out/v.split('#')[0]).exists(),(p,v)
     print(p.name,p.stat().st_size,'bytes;',len(imgs),'verified embedded images')
    checks=json.loads((out/'graphics-checks.json').read_text());assert all(not x['text_outside_canvas'] for x in checks)
    for name in ['results.json','boeing-descent-crosscheck.json']:
     d=json.loads((out/name).read_text())
     for p,expected in d['input_sha256'].items():
      assert hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()==expected,(name,p)
     print(name, 'input hashes verified')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(out/'conditional-hydroacoustic-tests.zip') as z:
     assert z.testzip() is None
     print('ZIP:',len(z.namelist()),'entries; CRC checks passed')
    print('Canvas checks:',len(checks),'figures passed')

── Assistant ───────────────────────────────────────────────
Fixed—the [report](/jackbox/home/MH370/.sources/kadri-2024-hydroacoustics/outputs/conditional-hypothesis-tests/report.html) and [graphics gallery](/jackbox/home/MH370/.sources/kadri-2024-hydroacoustics/outputs/conditional-hypothesis-tests/graphics.html) are now below the 5 MB limit. Full-resolution originals remain available.

The Boeing cross-check revealed an important limitation in our previous window:

| Conditional scenario | Time after 00:19:37 | Approximate impact UTC |
|---|---:|---|
| Previous high-altitude, constant-rate assumption | 58–139 seconds | 00:20:35–00:21:56 |
| Boeing final descending segment, no subsequent climb | 11–38 seconds | 00:19:48–00:20:16 |
| Boeing earlier high-rate segment followed by recovery | 77–161 seconds | 00:20:54–00:22:18 |

**Only the first row is a prior-conditional 95% interval.** The Boeing rows are illustrative envelopes obtained by aligning published descent-rate bounds with simulator samples—not complete BFO/system-timing fits. They include a short extrapolation because the exported records stop above water.

The shorter times arise chiefly because most final-segment alignments are **below our assumed 20,000 ft minimum**. “Recovery” also cannot be labelled definitively as a phugoid from altitude records alone.

Separately, ATSB reports some simulations remaining airborne about **20 minutes after second-engine flameout**. Under its nominal two-minute transmission delay, that means about **18 minutes after the final transmission**, roughly **00:37:30**—not a demonstrated 95% bound or a high-rate-compatible maximum. [ATSB report, pp. 7–8](https://www.atsb.gov.au/sites/default/files/media/5773389/ae-2014-054_mh370-search-and-debris-update_aug2017.pdf).

[View the new comparison, altitude histories and CL/DG reception windows](/jackbox/home/MH370/.sources/kadri-2024-hydroacoustics/outputs/conditional-hypothesis-tests/boeing-descent-crosscheck.html).

The acoustic searches have not yet been rerun using these additional timing alternatives.

      +    if not np.isfinite(value) or not np.all(np.isfinite(null)):

      +def complete_bank(b,keep,cl,dg,rng,interval_kind='prior95'):
      +    n=len(keep)
      +    b['dcl']=distance(b['lat'],b['lon'],'CL');b['ddg']=distance(b['lat'],b['lon'],'DG')
      +    for key,dkey,series in [('cl','dcl',cl),('dg','ddg',dg)]:
      +        b[key]=b['t0']+b[dkey]/rng.uniform(*CFG['celerity_km_s'],n)+rng.uniform(-CFG['station_timing_jitter_s'],CFG['station_timing_jitter_s'],n)
      +        b['i'+('c' if key=='cl' else 'd')]=np.rint((b[key]-series.time_epoch_s[0])/STEP).astype(int)
      +    cv=(b['ic']>=0)&(b['ic']<len(cl.values));dv=(b['id']>=0)&(b['id']<len(dg.values))
      +    cv &= cl.valid[np.clip(b['ic'],0,len(cl.values)-1)]
      +    dv &= dg.valid[np.clip(b['id'],0,len(dg.values)-1)]
      +    b['valid']=keep & cv & dv;b['keep']=keep;b['cv']=cv & keep;b['dv']=dv & keep
      +    b['pair_indices']=np.flatnonzero(b['valid'])
      +    assert b['pair_indices'].size>0, 'No paired coverage for this bank: do not interpret as silence'
      +    def report_interval(x):
      +        return quant(x) if interval_kind=='prior95' else [float(np.min(x)),float(np.median(x)),float(np.max(x))]
      +    summary={'cl_arrival_s':report_interval(b['cl'][keep]),'dg_arrival_s':report_interval(b['dg'][keep]),
      +             'cl_range_km':report_interval(b['dcl'][keep]),'dg_range_km':report_interval(b['ddg'][keep]),
      +             'arrival_lag_s':report_interval((b['dg']-b['cl'])[keep]),
      +             'impact_time_s':report_interval(b['t0'][keep]),'interval_kind':interval_kind,
      +             'CL_coverage_fraction':float(cv[keep].mean()),'DG_coverage_fraction':float(dv[keep].mean()),
      +             'joint_coverage_fraction':float(b['valid'].sum()/keep.sum()),'joint_sample_count':int(b['valid'].sum())}
      +    return b,summary

      +def expanded_banks(grids,cl,dg):
      +    """Timing-only alternatives: sampling allocation is not a physical case prior."""
      +    rng=np.random.default_rng(CFG['expanded_timing_seed']);n=CFG['samples_per_hypothesis']
      +    files,archive,arrays,rows,_,_=boeing.case_records()
      +    lat_arc,lon_arc=dense_arc()
      +    dl=np.sqrt(np.diff(lat_arc)**2+(np.diff(lon_arc)*np.cos(np.radians(lat_arc[:-1])))**2)
      +    along=np.r_[0,np.cumsum(dl)];u=rng.uniform(0,along[-1],n)
      +    lat=np.interp(u,along,lat_arc);lon=np.interp(u,along,lon_arc)
      +    tangent=np.arctan2(np.gradient(lon_arc)*np.cos(np.radians(lat_arc)),np.gradient(lat_arc))
      +    az=np.interp(u,along,tangent)+np.pi/2
      +    cross=rng.uniform(-CFG['expanded_arc_cross_track_nm'],CFG['expanded_arc_cross_track_nm'],n)*1852/6371008.8
      +    phi,lam=np.radians(lat),np.radians(lon)
      +    op=np.arcsin(np.sin(phi)*np.cos(cross)+np.cos(phi)*np.sin(cross)*np.cos(az))
      +    ol=lam+np.arctan2(np.sin(az)*np.sin(cross)*np.cos(phi),np.cos(cross)-np.sin(phi)*np.sin(op))
      +    spatial={'Arc':{'lat':np.degrees(op),'lon':np.degrees(ol)}}
      +    for label,g in grids.items():
      +        weights=g['probabilityMass']*g['hpd95'];ix=rng.choice(len(weights),n,p=weights/weights.sum())
      +        spatial[label]={'lat':g['latitude'][ix],'lon':g['longitude'][ix]}
      +    banks={};summaries={};templates=[]
      +    for short,branch in [('terminal','final_descending_leg'),('recovery','recovery_after_match'),('extended',None)]:
      +        if branch is None:
      +            delay=rng.uniform(*CFG['extended_impact_delay_s'],n);case=np.zeros(n,int)
      +            index=np.full(n,-1,int);accel=np.full(n,np.nan)
      +        else:
      +            selected=[dict(r,template_index=i) for i,r in enumerate(rows) if r['branch']==branch]
      +            counts={k:sum(r['case']==k for r in selected) for k in {r['case'] for r in selected}}
      +            weights=np.array([1/(len(counts)*counts[r['case']]) for r in selected])
      +            idx=rng.choice(len(selected),n,p=weights)
      +            delays=np.array([[r['projected_contact_delay_min_s'],r['projected_contact_delay_max_s']] for r in selected])
      +            delay=rng.uniform(delays[idx,0],delays[idx,1])
      +            case=np.array([r['case'] for r in selected])[idx]
      +            index=np.array([r['template_index'] for r in selected])[idx]
      +            accel=np.array([r['acceleration_8s_g'] for r in selected])[idx]
      +            templates.extend(selected)
      +        for location,xy in spatial.items():
      +            key=f'{location} {short}'
      +            b=dict(xy,t0=TREF+delay,boeing_case=case,template_index=index,acceleration_8s_g=accel)
      +            b,s=complete_bank(b,np.ones(n,bool),cl,dg,rng,'sampled_outer_envelope')
      +            s.update({'spatial_family':location,'timing_family':short,'sampling_status':'design allocation, not empirical case probabilities',
      +                      'impact_delay_s':[float(delay.min()),float(np.median(delay)),float(delay.max())]})
      +            banks[key]=b;summaries[key]=s
      +    save_csv('expanded-timing-templates.csv',templates)
      +    return banks,summaries,[*files,archive,Path(boeing.__file__)]

      +def family_maximum_controls(result):
      +    """Same surrogate realisation is searched across every declared alternative.
      +
      +    Maximum across source banks, DG treatments and major-burst treatments;
      +    Bonferroni factor two only for the two unlike statistics (J and Pearson r).
      +    The two null families are reported separately, never selected by smaller p.
      +    """
      +    pools={key:[] for key in ['joint_iaaft','joint_block','correlation_iaaft','correlation_block']}
      +    for prefix in ['', 'bearing-excluded-']:
      +        with np.load(OUT/(prefix+'null-controls.npz')) as a:
      +            for key in result:
      +                if (' / major-burst bearing veto' in key) != bool(prefix):continue
      +                stem=key.removesuffix(' / major-burst bearing veto').replace(' / ','_')
      +                for field,p in [('joint_iaaft',''),('joint_block','blocks_'),('correlation_iaaft','corr_'),('correlation_block','blockcorr_')]:
      +                    pools[field].append(a[p+stem])
      +    maxima={k:np.nanmax(np.array(v),axis=0) for k,v in pools.items()}
      +    for row in result.values():
      +        for key,values in maxima.items():
      +            observed=row['joint_score' if key.startswith('joint') else 'correlation_r']
      +            p=pvalue(values,observed)
      +            row[key+'_global_p']=p
      +            row[key+'_global_two_statistic_p']=min(1,2*p) if np.isfinite(p) else float('nan')
      +    np.savez_compressed(OUT/'whole-experiment-null-controls.npz',**maxima)
      +    return {'method':'paired-realisation maximum across every bank and treatment; factor two across statistics',
      +            'search_variants_per_statistic':len(result),'statistics':2,
      +            'minimum_resolvable_two_statistic_p':2/(CFG['null_replicates']+1),
      +            'null_maximum_q95':{k:float(np.quantile(v,.95)) for k,v in maxima.items()}}
      +

      +def expanded_summary(d):
      +    rows=[];coverage=[]
      +    for label in d['expanded_hypotheses']:
      +        s=d['hypotheses'][label]
      +        coverage.append(f"| {label} | {times(s['impact_time_s'])} | {times(s['cl_arrival_s'])} | {times(s['dg_arrival_s'])} | {s['joint_coverage_fraction']:.1%} |")
      +        for method in ['unfiltered','filtered']:
      +            key=label+' / '+method+' / major-burst bearing veto';r=d['tests'][key]
      +            rows.append(f"| {label} / {method} | {c.utc(r['CL_actual_score_peak_s'])} | {c.utc(r['DG_actual_score_peak_s'])} | {r['correlation_r']:.3f} | {r['joint_iaaft_global_two_statistic_p']:.3f} / {r['joint_block_global_two_statistic_p']:.3f} | {r['correlation_iaaft_global_two_statistic_p']:.3f} / {r['correlation_block_global_two_statistic_p']:.3f} |")
      +    new=[(k,r) for k,r in d['tests'].items() if k.split(' / ')[0] in d['expanded_hypotheses']]
      +    count=sum(max(r['joint_iaaft_global_two_statistic_p'],r['joint_block_global_two_statistic_p'])<.05 or
      +              max(r['correlation_iaaft_global_two_statistic_p'],r['correlation_block_global_two_statistic_p'])<.05 for k,r in new)
      +    best=min(new,key=lambda kr:max(kr[1]['correlation_iaaft_global_two_statistic_p'],kr[1]['correlation_block_global_two_statistic_p']))
      +    diagnostic='No added search passes the 5% whole-experiment threshold under both null families.' if count==0 else f'{count} added variants pass the declared threshold under both null families; these still require raw-array source verification.'
      +    osc=[]
      +    for timing in ['terminal','recovery','extended']:
      +        key='OSCAR '+timing+' / filtered / major-burst bearing veto';r=d['tests'][key]
      +        osc.append(f"### OSCAR {timing}\n\nA timing-compatible example places impact at approximately {c.utc(r['source_time_s'])}, with score peaks near CL {c.utc(r['CL_actual_score_peak_s'])} and DG {c.utc(r['DG_actual_score_peak_s'])}. This is a selected alignment, not a recovered impact time.\n\n![OSCAR {timing} selected pair](best-surviving-pair-oscar-{timing}.png)")
      +    return f'''# Rerun with Boeing timing alternatives
      +
      +15 September 2026 · All event times UTC, 8 March 2014
      +
      +[All new comparison panels](expanded-timing-graphics.html) · [Full report](report.html) · [Timing derivation](boeing-descent-crosscheck.html) · [Download package](conditional-hydroacoustic-tests.zip)
      +
      +## Result
      +
      +**{diagnostic}** The expanded search distinguishes a genuine plotted fluctuation from evidence that CL and DG received the same aircraft impact. Remaining DG airgun structure still offers many possible alignments. A significant pressure excursion at one station, or an IAAFT-only result, is not by itself evidence of a common source.
      +
      +The smallest robust, whole-experiment correlation p-value among the added variants is **{max(best[1]['correlation_iaaft_global_two_statistic_p'],best[1]['correlation_block_global_two_statistic_p']):.3f}** ({best[0]}; r={best[1]['correlation_r']:.3f}). Here “robust” only means taking the larger result from our two specified null families; it is not a guarantee against unmodelled artefacts.
      +
      +## Exactly what was rerun
      +
      +We added **12 source/timing banks**, each with 250,000 samples: three timing alternatives crossed with the Arc, BRAN, OSCAR and GLORYS spatial controls. The original four banks were rerun too: 4 million source/timing draws in total. For all sixteen, we repeated the joint-excursion and time-shift correlation tests using both unfiltered DG and the preservation-constrained rank-{d['filter_rank']} filter, with and without excluding the two major CL bursts. This makes **64 search variants per statistic**, not 64 independent observations.
      +
      +- **Terminal:** only the discrete Boeing alignment episodes whose subsequent exported altitude descends monotonically. Including explicit final-slope completion, the available delays span approximately 11–38 s.
      +- **Recovery:** earlier high-rate episodes followed by altitude recovery, with delays spanning approximately 77–161 s. This is not a verified phugoid classification.
      +- **Extended:** uniform numerical coverage from zero to eighteen minutes after 00:19:37.443. This analyst-selected sensitivity window is motivated by the broader ATSB duration context, not supplied as a Boeing probability distribution or maximum glide bound. It is not restricted to the isolated +18-minute example shown previously.
      +
      +For the Boeing branches we allocate equal computational samples to each case, then to its eligible alignment rows, and cover the small interval between the 1/5/10-second completion alternatives. Those allocations are **not empirical probabilities**. The screen remains the same loose two-rate-bound screen as the preceding comparison; acceleration-threshold and full joint-BFO fitting are not newly imposed in this acoustic rerun.
      +
      +The arc control covers 20°S–45°S, with uniform arclength and a ±100 NM cross-track offset. It is **not** a reconstruction of each Boeing trajectory's geographic displacement or a feasible-flight posterior. Pléiades uses each existing transport PDF inside its own 95% highest-density region, separately. Source time and location are independent within each declared search design. Boeing timing is transferred to those locations as a conditional test, without asserting that all combinations are flight-dynamically possible.
      +
      +## Reception coverage
      +
      +The new intervals are **sampled outer envelopes**, not 95% intervals; some contain gaps between discrete timing episodes. Coverage fractions measure how much of the allocated search has both receptions in the guarded published drawings. They are not probabilities of detection or MH370 membership.
      +
      +| Search | Impact UTC envelope | CL UTC envelope | DG UTC envelope | Paired coverage |
      +|---|---|---|---|---|
      +{chr(10).join(coverage)}
      +
      +![Expanded reception coverage](expanded-timing-coverage.png)
      +
      +## What counts as unusual after trying all these alternatives?
      +
      +We generated {d['configuration']['null_replicates']} controls under each of two background models. IAAFT preserves CL pressure amplitudes and approximately its spectrum but changes phases. The other permutes 60-second blocks, preserving actual bursts. DG stays fixed, including its remaining interference. Every control repeats the full source-bank and lag search. The same surrogate realisation is used across all banks, DG treatments and major-burst treatments, allowing a **whole-experiment maximum** instead of selecting the most attractive p-value from separate runs.
      +
      +For statistic S (joint score J or correlation r), null family N and replicate b:
      +
      +\\[
      +M_b^{{(S,N)}}=\\max_{{h,d,v}}S_b(h,d,v),\\qquad
      +p_{{\\mathrm{{global}}}}^{{(S,N)}}=\\frac{{1+\\sum_b\\mathbf1[M_b^{{(S,N)}}\\ge S_{{\\mathrm{{observed}}}}]}}{{B+1}}.
      +\\]
      +
      +We report \\(\\min(1,2p_{{\\mathrm{{global}}}})\\) to account for trying the two unlike statistics. The two null families are shown separately; requiring both to pass avoids selecting the more favourable background assumption. The finite-control resolution is {d['whole_experiment_controls']['minimum_resolvable_two_statistic_p']:.3f} after this factor two, not zero. This correction covers the present experiment, **not every historical analysis or selection of the published drawings**. The more conservative 128-comparison Bonferroni columns are also retained in the CSV for transparency.
      +
      +![Expanded lag correlations](expanded-timing-correlations.png)
      +
      +The table below shows the nuisance-exclusion sensitivity. Times are the score peaks selected for the **joint-excursion** test; the maximum r is from the separate lag scan and need not refer to that same pair. Each p-value pair is **IAAFT / burst-preserving blocks**, fully adjusted for this experiment.
      +
      +| Search / DG treatment | CL score peak | DG score peak | Max r | Joint global p | Correlation global p |
      +|---|---|---|---:|---|---|
      +{chr(10).join(rows)}
      +
      +Excluding 00:51:45–00:52:20 and 00:54:10–00:54:50 is only a nuisance sensitivity. In particular, the **extended arc corridor can include the preferred 306.18° candidate's implied location and impact time**; its removal cannot be justified there as a universal bearing contradiction. The unexcluded results are retained in [the complete table](hypothesis-tests.csv), alongside all other tests. Imported catalogue bearings are not new measurements, and an adjacent time label does not prove waveform identity.
      +
      +## Selected OSCAR comparison panels
      +
      +{chr(10).join(osc)}
      +
      +Equivalent panels for all twelve added banks are in [the new gallery](expanded-timing-graphics.html). They plot grey unfiltered DG behind purple filtered DG, preserve data gaps, and annotate the implied source time, coordinates and adjusted p-values. Coordinates identify one sampled timing-compatible construction, not a unique source estimate.
      +
      +## What has not changed
      +
      +The source-coupling working hypothesis remains 10⁻⁶–10⁻⁴ of normal kinetic energy. These searches do not gate on predicted pressure or fit impact energy; the existing pressure plots remain attached to the **original constant-rate energy model**, not newly inferred Boeing impact states. No new hydrodynamic, bathymetric, AGW Green-function or calibrated station-response calculation has been performed.
      +
      +The analysis still uses filtered, plotting-decimated publication vectors—not raw hydrophone arrays. It cannot measure a new bearing or calibrate a detection probability. Missing or inconspicuous plotted signals cannot exclude a high-energy impact. No acoustic likelihood or integrated-estimator weight has been created.
      +
      +## Reproduction
      +
      +Run `conditional_hypothesis_tests.py`, then `boeing_descent_crosscheck.py`, then `conditional_hypothesis_report.py` from the source README. Seeds, all input/code hashes, software versions, runtime and memory are in [results.json](results.json). The numerical run passed {len(d['checks_passed'])} focused checks. New templates: [CSV](expanded-timing-templates.csv). Source citations and the full mathematical definitions are in [methods.html](methods.html).

      $ .venv/bin/python -B - <<'PY'
    from pathlib import Path
    import base64,hashlib,io,json,re,zipfile
    from PIL import Image
    out=Path('.sources/kadri-2024-hydroacoustics/outputs/conditional-hypothesis-tests')
    for p in sorted(out.glob('*.html')):
     t=p.read_text();assert p.stat().st_size<4_500_000
     imgs=re.findall(r'src="data:image/(jpeg|png);base64,(["'^"]+)"'"',t)
     for typ,v in imgs:
      with Image.open(io.BytesIO(base64.b64decode(v))) as im:im.verify()
     for v in re.findall(r'(?:href|src)="(["'^"]+)"'"',t):
      if ':' not in v and not v.startswith('#'):assert (out/v.split('#')[0]).exists(),(p,v)
     print(p.name, p.stat().st_size, 'bytes; images',len(imgs))
    for name in ['results.json','boeing-descent-crosscheck.json']:
     d=json.loads((out/name).read_text())
     for p,expected in d['input_sha256'].items():assert hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()==expected,(name,p)
     print(name,'all input hashes match')
    checks=json.loads((out/'graphics-checks.json').read_text());assert len(checks)==33 and all(not r['text_outside_canvas'] for r in checks)
    print('Canvas checks:',len(checks),'passed')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(out/'conditional-hydroacoustic-tests.zip') as z:
     assert z.testzip() is None
     for p in out.iterdir():
      if p.name in z.namelist():assert z.read(p.name)==p.read_bytes(),p
     print('ZIP verified',len(z.namelist()),'entries')

**No convincing common-impact signal emerged under both noise models.** The shorter Pléiades windows select CL features near 00:43:33 and 00:47:10; importantly, the latter is an edge-censored tail, not a separately resolved event. The extended window returns to the feature near 00:49:41.

The largest added correlation was approximately **0.282**, but was not exceptional after accounting for the full search.

This does **not** reject the impact hypotheses: coverage remains incomplete, DG interference persists, and coupling is uncalibrated. These were timing-search reruns; the original energy/coupling assumptions remain unchanged.

Both viewing pages are below 5 MB.
